## Review

**6 out of 10, Needs substantial work.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck has a strong, consistent identity: black ground, green accent, large quoted headlines, and polished photography and renders (slides 1, 13, 31). But the body paragraphs on slides 7 to 28 are tiny and low contrast, so the supporting explanation is unreadable at any normal viewing size. The 76-page route is also weak. Slides 32 to 43 are photo grids with no message, and slides 45 to 72 repeat earlier content as two-up thumbnails with text too small to read, some with near-empty chart frames (slide 57). The core message can be recovered from the headline quotes, but the deck needs real editing.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the dark, green-accent system, the large quote headlines with attribution, the clean cost-per-token chart on slide 13 with its labeled 2X, 3X and 10X callouts, and the stat strip on slide 31.

**Most useful repair:** Raise body text to a readable size and contrast, cut the repeated two-up slides 45 to 72 or rebuild them as single full-size slides, and fix the empty chart frames on slide 57.

**What would change the score:** Condense the photo montages and the quote walls (slides 29 and 30) into a few purposeful pages so the argument from slides 7 to 28 reaches the close without interruption.

_Confidence medium. 72 of 76 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

This recap of NVIDIA's GTC 2026 event in San Jose opens with a title slide and a series of press and analyst quotes framing the event as a major moment for AI. The body then moves through the company's argument in order: AI as a five-layer system from energy to applications, the shift from training to inference, the economics of cost per token and tokens per watt, the Vera Rubin platform and Vera CPU, Dynamo and Groq for inference orchestration, agentic AI with OpenClaw and NemoClaw, open models and sovereign AI, the DSX framework for AI factories, and physical AI in robotics and autonomous driving. Several slides use large quoted headlines and short paragraphs to tie each section to a single idea. Event statistics such as keynote views and attendance appear mid-deck, and analyst and press reactions fill the later slides. The deck closes with a summary slide that restates the platform and the claim that agentic and physical AI are just beginning. Slides 7, 10, 12 and 13 are good examples of headline-driven structure with figures carrying the argument.

## Questions about this deck

**How many slides does the NVIDIA GTC 2026 Highlights deck have?**

The NVIDIA GTC 2026 Highlights deck has 76 slides. It opens with a title slide and press and analyst quotes, then moves through the company's argument in order, ending with a summary slide on the platform.

**What does the NVIDIA GTC 2026 deck say about the five layers of AI?**

Slide 8 says AI depends on five layers: energy, chips, infrastructure, models, and applications, and that all five need to scale together. It adds that if one layer stalls, the system stalls.

**What does the NVIDIA GTC 2026 deck say about demand visibility for Blackwell and Rubin?**

Slide 10 states that one year ago NVIDIA saw roughly $500 billion of demand, and that today visibility is at least $1 trillion through 2027. It says this reflects Blackwell and Rubin only.

**What performance gains does the NVIDIA GTC 2026 deck attribute to Dynamo?**

Slide 14 says Dynamo boosts inference performance by up to 7x, lowering token cost and increasing revenue. The same slide says that with Blackwell and GB300 NVL72, it drives up to 50x performance gains.

### Slide 5

**Text**

KO / K5 Invited Guests / Reserved / Guests

**Layout**

Full-slide 2x2 grid of four flush photographs with thin black gutters of about 0.4% between them and black margins at the edges. There is no title, footer, or slide number; the four images fill the frame edge to edge.

**Visuals**

Four photographs of audiences and event guests at an NVIDIA GTC event. Top-left: a dim blue-violet auditorium with rows of seated attendees; a silver-haired woman with glasses in a dark jacket is centred, with a man in a dark blazer beside her and an attendee holding a phone at left. Top-right: a backstage or wings scene under dark rigging; a man in a dark suit embraces a man in a black leather jacket with silver hair, with other people blurred in the background. Bottom-left: three men in suits seated in green-and-black theatre seats, one gesturing with a raised hand, with green lanyards; a green seat placard reads 'KO / K5 Invited Guests' and 'Reserved'. Bottom-right: a row of seated men in dark blazers and a light checked shirt, several clapping, one holding a phone aloft, lit in purple-blue light with green lanyards visible. Each photo is a rectangle, not cropped to a shape, with no overlay or tint beyond the existing stage lighting.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 background. Pure black slide background with four rectangular photographs separated by black gutters. ### Slide 6

**Text**

“This is GTC, the AI festival of everything that drives the tech world.” / Jim Cramer, CNBC

**Layout**

Full-bleed photograph fills the whole slide with no margins. A single centred quote sits in the lower fifth of the slide, spanning about 9% to 91% of the width, with the green opening and closing quotation marks flanking the white quote line. The attribution is centred on a line beneath it. No title, footer, logo or slide number is present.

**Visuals**

One full-bleed photograph of a large arena stage during a keynote. A presenter in a dark jacket stands small on a dark multi-level stage at centre-right, facing a huge illuminated LED screen behind him. The screen shows a daytime render of a modern campus of glass-walled buildings with curved white roofs, a road, lawns, trees and hedges, under a pale blue sky with white clouds. The foreground is a dense audience seen from above and to the left, lit in cool blue and white, with a few raised phones visible. Rows of white stage lights hang along the top edge. Lighting: bright screen and spotlights against a dark room. Dominant colours: black, deep blue, sky blue and white. The photo is uncropped apart from full bleed, with a dark gradient overlay over the lower half that darkens the audience behind the quote. The quote sits on a dark translucent band.

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed photograph of an arena stage and audience over a black #000000 base. Black background with a full-bleed photograph. Quote text is white #FFFFFF in NVIDIA Sans Medium, a geometric sans-serif, at about 6.5% of slide height. Quotation marks are green #76B900 in NVIDIA Sans Regular. Attribution is grey #A6A6A6 in NVIDIA Sans Regular at about 2.6% of slide height. Accent colour is NVIDIA green #76B900. No title, no shapes with borders, no corner radii, no shadows.

### Slide 7

**Review notes**

- Review: But the body paragraphs on slides 7 to 28 are tiny and low contrast, so the supporting explanation is unreadable at any normal viewing size.
- What would change the score: Condense the photo montages and the quote walls (slides 29 and 30) into a few purposeful pages so the argument from slides 7 to 28 reaches the close without interruption.

**Text**

BREAKTHROUGHS
ECOSYSTEMS
DEVELOPERS
20 YEARS
CUDA
FLYWHEEL
INSTALLED
BASE
“CUDA has over 4 million developers,
3,000+ optimized applications, and deep
integration into every major AI framework.”
Forbes
For 20 years, CUDA has built the developer flywheel powering accelerated computing.
CUDA-X turns algorithms into infrastructure across AI, data, and science, compounding
performance at scale.
As compute grows, software drives efficiency—defining throughput, cost per token,
and how much intelligence the world can produce.

**Layout**

Two-column split on a black slide: a circular four-node cycle diagram occupies the left half (about x 11 to 47 percent, y 14 to 81 percent) and a left-aligned text column starts at x 56.8 percent and runs to about x 93 percent, spanning y 28 to 80 percent. There is no title placeholder, footer, logo or slide number, and no visible frame or margin rules.

**Visuals**

One hand-drawn cycle diagram in white chalk-style strokes with four node labels (BREAKTHROUGHS top, DEVELOPERS right, INSTALLED BASE bottom, ECOSYSTEMS left) and a green hand-lettered centre label reading 20 YEARS / CUDA / FLYWHEEL. Four curved white sketched arrows connect the nodes clockwise: BREAKTHROUGHS to ECOSYSTEMS (arrowhead at ECOSYSTEMS), ECOSYSTEMS to INSTALLED BASE (arrowhead at INSTALLED BASE), INSTALLED BASE to DEVELOPERS (arrowhead at DEVELOPERS), DEVELOPERS to BREAKTHROUGHS (arrowhead at BREAKTHROUGHS). No chart axes, legend or highlighted points; no photographs or logos.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 fill covering the whole slide. Pure black background with no gradient or texture. Quote text and node labels are white #FFFFFF; the centre label, quote marks and accent are NVIDIA green #76B900; attribution is grey #A6A6A6; body text is grey #A1A1A1. Typefaces: NVIDIA Sans (grotesque sans-serif), Medium for the quote and Regular for the attribution and body; the node labels and centre label are hand-lettered chalk-style caps. Quote text is about 4.6 percent of slide height, attribution and body about 2.3 to 2.6 percent. Diagram strokes are white sketchy lines with no shadows, corner radii or shapes behind them.

### Slide 8

_Long standard legal text on this slide is summarised, not transcribed; its exact words are in the measured list below._

**Text**

“AI depends on five layers—energy, / chips, infrastructure, models, and / applications—and that all five need / to scale together.” / Fortune / AI does not scale in isolation. It scales as a system. Energy powers chips, / chips power infrastructure, infrastructure runs models, and models / enable applications. Each layer compounds the next. If one stalls, the / system stalls. AI factories demand all five layers advance together. / APPLICATIONS / MODELS / INFRASTRUCTURE / CHIPS / ENERGY / CHATBOTS / DIGITAL BIOLOGY / ROBOTAXI / ENTERPRISE / AI AGENTS / SCIENCE / ROBOTICS / MANUFACTURING / AI CODER / LLM / VLM / VLA / MMLLM / GPT / DM / GNN / MOE / SSM / LBM / AI FACTORIES

**Layout**

Two zones on a black slide. The left column holds the pull quote, its attribution and a body paragraph, left-aligned from about 7% of the width. The right area holds a vertical stack of five labelled layer boxes with curly brackets on their right, a 4 by 2 grid of application icons with labels, two rows of ten model-type tags, and an AI factories illustration at bottom right. Content sits between about 21% and 85% of the height; no title placeholder, footer, page number or logo is visible.

**Visuals**

Hand-drawn chalk-style sketch illustration in white and gold. Five stacked rounded rectangles with white hand-drawn outlines and no fill, labelled from top to bottom APPLICATIONS, MODELS, INFRASTRUCTURE, CHIPS and ENERGY. Each has a right-side curly brace; the top brace spans APPLICATIONS, the middle brace spans MODELS, the lower brace spans INFRASTRUCTURE, CHIPS and ENERGY. Application icons in a 4 by 2 grid: row 1 CHATBOTS (a speech bubble with a gold arrow), DIGITAL BIOLOGY (a gold molecular strand), ROBOTAXI (a white car with gold accents), ENTERPRISE AI AGENTS (a robot with stacked gold cubes); row 2 SCIENCE (a gold atom), ROBOTICS (a humanoid robot with raised arm), MANUFACTURING (a robotic arm), AI CODER (a laptop window showing a code symbol with a gold chip). Model tags are small rounded rectangles with thin white outlines and white capitals: row 1 LLM, VLM, VLA, MMLLM, GPT; row 2 DM, GNN, MOE, SSM, LBM. Below them, the label AI FACTORIES above a sketched illustration of three stacked tiers of server racks in a pyramid, drawn in white outline with gold-lit rack units, widest at the bottom. No photographs; no cropping; no overlays.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 covering the whole slide. Black background (#000000). Pull quote in white (#FFFFFF) NVIDIA Sans Medium, sans-serif, about 4.6% of slide height, with green (#76B900) opening and closing quotation marks. Attribution Fortune in grey (#A6A6A6) NVIDIA Sans Regular about 2.6%. Body paragraph in grey (#A1A1A1) NVIDIA Sans Regular about 2.3%, line spacing about 3.4% of height. Diagram labels and tags in white (#FFFFFF) light sans-serif capitals. Accent colour green (#76B900) for quote marks and gold (about #C9A227) highlights in illustrations. Outlines are white hand-drawn sketch strokes, slightly irregular, about 0.3% of height; no shadows, gradients or corner-radius beyond the sketched rounding.

**Layout**

Two-column split on a full black slide. The left column (about 0 to 60 percent of the width) holds a large logo-and-company map that runs from just below the top edge to the bottom edge; the right column (starting at about 64 percent of the width) holds a quote block vertically centred around 28 to 45 percent of the height, then a body paragraph at about 57 to 72 percent of the height. The quote and body text are left-aligned on a shared left edge at about 65 percent of the width. No title in the conventional sense, no footer, no slide number, no logo outside the map.

**Visuals**

A dense logo map (single raster image, about 1500 by 826 px) on black. Top: the green NVIDIA eye logo with the wordmark NVIDIA in white, a red heart emoji, and the words AI Natives in white sans-serif. Below, a row of six grey-outlined rounded panels containing small white logo wordmarks grouped by label: AI for Auto (4 rows of logos), AI for Customer Support, AI for Engineering, AI for Healthcare, AI for Robotics, AI for Search (4 logos), AI for Software Development. Second band of three panels on the left (DL Frameworks, Inference Frameworks) and four panels (Agent Frameworks/Protocols, Frontier Model Builders, Model to Production) as listed in text. Lower: a gold-outlined dark rounded box titled NVIDIA AI Platform with a gold pill label at its top; inside, a row of 12 gold-outlined rounded tiles (cuDNN, Magnum IO, cuOpt, cuVS, cuDF, Omniverse, TRT-LLM, Dynamo, NeMo, PhysicsNeMo, Cosmos, Nemotron) above a row of seven gold line-drawn hardware icons with labels: server-chassis outline (NVIDIA Hopper), long flat card with dot pattern (NVIDIA RTX PRO Blackwell Server Edition), tall rack server (NVIDIA Blackwell), hexagonal shield-chip icon (Confidential Computing), tall rack server (NVIDIA Rubin), network card (NVIDIA ConnectX and BlueField Networking), flat switch module (NVIDIA Quantum IB, Spectrum-X Networking). Below the gold box, a faded, blurred duplicate strip of the same labels, mostly unreadable, cut off at the bottom edge. Right of the map is a narrow vertical strip (about 2.4 percent wide) that shades from black to dark blue glow at its lower part. The quote marks are green. Lighting is flat, no photograph.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 with a faint blue glow along the bottom edge. Pure black #000000 background across the whole slide, with a faint blue glow at the bottom edge near the map. Quote text in white #FFFFFF NVIDIASans Medium, sans-serif, about 4.6 percent of slide height per line, normal weight, tight line spacing. Opening and closing quote marks in NVIDIA green #76B900. Attribution Business Insider in grey #A6A6A6 NVIDIASans Regular, about 2.6 percent of height. Body paragraph in grey #A1A1A1 NVIDIASans Regular, about 2.3 percent of height, about 3.4 percent line pitch. Map uses white labels, gold #C2A36B outlines and labels for the platform box, and grey panel outlines #3A3A3A. No shadows, no gradients on text, rounded corners on tiles.

“$1 trillion in sales as new computing era begins.”
The Wall Street Journal
One year ago, we saw roughly $500 billion of demand. Today, that visibility is at least $1 trillion through 2027.
This is not speculative. It is infrastructure being built—across hyperscalers, model builders, sovereign clouds, and enterprises. And this only reflects Blackwell and Rubin. The AI factory buildout is just beginning.
2027 is a step-function in AI factory scale.

**Layout**

Two-column composition on a full black field with no visible title bar, footer or slide number. The left column holds a hand-drawn bar chart (x about 3 to 59 percent) and the centre column a hand-drawn pie chart (about 35 to 60 percent of the width), both sitting in the upper-middle band of the slide. The right text column starts at x 64.4 percent and is vertically centred from about y 33 to 83 percent, left-aligned on a common left edge. Margins are about 3 percent on the left and roughly 5 percent on the right.

**Visuals**

One sketch-style illustration spanning x 3.1 to 59.1 percent and y 16.3 to 81.0 percent, containing two hand-drawn charts. Chart 1 is a vertical bar chart titled BLACKWELL + RUBIN with two bars in bright green #76B900 with darker green shading and a rough charcoal-grey outline. Bar 1 labelled $0.5T sits over the bar, with category labels GTC DC 2025 and 2025-2026 beneath it; bar 2 is tall and labelled $1T+ above and STILL GROWING beneath that label, with category labels GTC SJ 2026 and 2025-2027 beneath it. The bars sit on a thin white horizontal baseline with small tick marks; no numeric axes or ranges are printed. A white curly brace points from the upper bar region to a list in green and white: ANTHROPIC, METASL, MULTIPLE OSS (green, labelled NEW SINCE GTC DC 2025 in dimmer green beside it), then XAI, GEMINI, OPENAI in white. Chart 2 is a hand-drawn pie chart of two green wedges, with the larger left wedge labelled 60% and the right wedge labelled 40% in black bold type; the wedges are separated by a dark gap, outlined in white, with jagged green edges. Leader lines connect CUDA IN CLOUD AI NATIVES (top), NCP SCC SOVEREIGN AI INDUSTRIAL ENTERPRISE (right) and HYPERSCALERS (bottom) to their wedges. The illustration is unboxed on the black background, with no photographic content.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 across the whole slide. Black full-bleed background, dark, high-contrast minimalist style. Headline quote in white NVIDIA Sans Medium (sans-serif, medium weight) with green #76B900 opening and closing quote marks. Attribution in grey #A6A6A6 regular weight. Body paragraphs in grey #A1A1A1 regular weight, sans-serif. Headline is about 4.6 percent of slide height; body about 2.3 percent; attribution about 2.6 percent. Chart labels are in a thin hand-lettered sans-serif in white and green. Chart bars and wedges are flat green with sketch-like rough edges and white or charcoal outlines; no shadows, no gradients, no rounded corners, no visible card or panel shapes.

### Slide 13

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Frames tokens per second per watt as the revenue metric, using a short three-line argument that builds to one clear claim.
- Review: The deck has a strong, consistent identity: black ground, green accent, large quoted headlines, and polished photography and renders (slides 1, 13, 31).
- Worth keeping: Keep the dark, green-accent system, the large quote headlines with attribution, the clean cost-per-token chart on slide 13 with its labeled 2X, 3X and 10X callouts, and the stat strip on slide 31.

### Slide 14

**Text**

NVIDIA Dynamo / Prefill / Input / KV Cache / 1st Token / Remaining Tokens / Decode / Vera Rubin NVL72 / Groq 3 LPX / KV CACHE / PREFILL / DECODE ATTN / ACTIVATIONS / DECODE FFN / TOKENS / “Dynamo boosts inference / performance by up to 7x, lowering / token cost and increasing revenue.” / Stock Titan / NVIDIA Dynamo is the operating system of the AI factory. With Blackwell / and GB300 NVL72, it drives up to 50x performance gains. / Integrated into an open ecosystem, it ensures maximum / throughput and efficiency.

**Layout**

Three-part horizontal composition on a full black slide: a left diagram block (x about 3 to 25%), a centre rack illustration pair (x about 29 to 60%), and a right text column starting at x 64.4% with the quote and body copy, all vertically centred between about y 23% and 85%. No title in the reading-order sense; the quote acts as the headline, placed top-right of the text column at y 28.3%. Text is left-aligned in the right column with a consistent left edge at x 65%. No footer, no slide number visible.

**Visuals**

Left: a black-line schematic titled 'NVIDIA Dynamo' in light beige-grey text. An arrow labelled 'Input' points right into a square chip icon labelled 'Prefill' (a gold-framed square with a grid of small dark squares). From the Prefill chip, a line labelled 'KV Cache' runs straight down, with an arrow into a second identical chip labelled 'Decode' below. From Prefill, a curved elbow arrow runs right to a row of five small squares: the first is solid white and labelled '1st Token' beneath, the other four are dark grey (#5F5F5F) and labelled 'Remaining Tokens' above a brace; a line from the Decode chip runs right and up with an arrow into the brace. Centre: two tall black server racks in outline with dark grey frames. Left rack labelled 'Vera Rubin NVL72' with the blocks stacked vertically: a top grey-gold vent unit, a green (#76B900) block labelled 'PREFILL', a grey middle section, a green block labelled 'DECODE ATTN' at the bottom. A curved white arrow on the left side labelled 'KV CACHE' points down into the rack. Between racks, two horizontal arrows: upper arrow pointing right labelled 'ACTIVATIONS', lower arrow pointing left. Right rack labelled 'Groq 3 LPX' with a top vent unit and one tall green (#76B900) block labelled 'DECODE FFN'. A right-pointing arrow labelled 'TOKENS' leaves the right rack. Racks have grey plinths at the bottom. Lighting: dark, studio-style, metallic highlights on the rack tops.

Background: Solid black #000000 covering the entire slide.

“The Inference King has been crowned.”
SemiAnalysis
GB300 NVL72 redefines the economics of inference. SemiAnalysis’
InferenceX testing shows performance per watt improving by nearly

Higher throughput within a fixed power envelope lowers cost per
token and expands revenue.
Cost per token defines leadership.

### Slide 16

**Text**

“Vera Rubin is 10x more efficient than its predecessor.”
CNBC
Vera Rubin is not just an NVL72 rack. It is a full AI factory pod. Blackwell systems scaled at the rack level. Rubin scales at the pod level—integrating compute, memory,
networking, and software into a single production unit. This shift increases throughput, reduces cost per token, and redefines how AI factories are built.

**Layout**

Centered single-column text stack in the upper part of the slide: large quote headline at top, attribution line beneath it, then a two-line centred body paragraph. A single dark silhouette of a person walks near the left-hand racks. Below the racks is a dark stage floor with thin bright white light strips along the front edge of the rack base, and a faint dark audience with scattered small points of light at the bottom edge. Full-bleed, unboxed, no overlay or tint.

### Slide 17

**Text**

NVIDIA Vera Rubin / 40,000,000X -10 years
“Vera Rubin will deliver 10x higher / inference throughput per watt and / roughly one-tenth the cost per token.” / Melius
Vera Rubin is a fully integrated AI computing system. Hardware and / software are co-designed end to end, operating as one architecture for / reasoning and agentic AI at scale.
Seven chips work together as one system: CPU, GPU, LPU, NVLink, / SuperNIC, DPU, and Spectrum-X. This level of integration defines / performance, efficiency, and AI factory economics. NVIDIA’s platform / spans CUDA-X, DSX infrastructure, and Vera Rubin systems.

**Layout**

Two-zone split: a full-height photograph fills the left 58% of the slide, and a black text column sits in the right portion starting at about 64% of the slide width. The quote is the top of the right column at about 28% from the top, with attribution beneath it, then two body paragraphs stacked with a gap, ending near 85% of the height. Left-aligned throughout, with a left margin of about 65% and a right margin of about 2% for the quote. No title bar, footer, slide number or logo is visible.

**Visuals**

One photograph and a second, narrower photograph. Photo 1 (left, full height, 0 to 58.4% wide): a man with swept grey hair and dark-framed glasses, wearing a black leather jacket over a black shirt, speaks while standing behind a dark stage counter. On the counter lie server trays tilted at an angle, showing exposed circuit boards, heatsinks and connectors in dark grey and black metal with some copper and orange-brown accents. Above and behind him a projection screen shows white sans-serif text in a perspective skew: 'NVIDIA Vera Rubin' on the upper line, larger, and '40,000,000X -10 years' below it, smaller. The composition is low-angle with the subject in the left third. The lighting is dark stage lighting with spot highlights on the hardware and the speaker. The dominant colours are black, charcoal grey and warm copper. The photo is full-bleed to the top and bottom edges and is not boxed. Photo 2 (overlapping the first, 43.3 to 64% wide, nearly full height): a portrait-format close-up of a second server tray lying at a slant on the stage, with dark grey chassis, exposed boards and a tall copper-coloured cable at its lower left. It is cropped at the bottom edge of the slide and blends into the black background. No charts, tables, diagrams or logos.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 fill covering the entire slide, behind both photographs. Black full-bleed background (#000000) throughout. Attribution 'Melius' is light grey (#A6A6A6) NVIDIASans Regular at about 2.6%. Body paragraphs are mid-grey (#A1A1A1) NVIDIASans Regular at about 2.3% of slide height, with generous line spacing of about 3.4% of height between lines and a blank line between paragraphs. Accent colour is NVIDIA green #76B900, used only for the quote marks. No shapes, rules, shadows, corner radii or gradients.

**Text**

“NVIDIA reinvents the CPU / for the age of agentic AI.” / SiliconANGLE / Vera is our first fully custom data center CPU. Photograph-style render of a tall black server rack (Vera CPU rack) centred slightly left of middle, front three-quarter view with its door open, showing a lit beige-gold grille of stacked compute trays and a second black rack with horizontal slatted vents behind it on the right. ### Slide 19

**Text**

Rubin GPU
288 GB HBM4
22 TB/s
50 PFLOPs (NVFP4)
336B Transistors
+ 2.5T (HBM4)
Groq 3 LPU
4 GB SRAM
1,200 TB/s SRAM Bandwidth
55X
9.6 PFLOPs (FP8)
784B Transistors
“Groq will play a role similar to Mellanox, extending the architecture as an ‘accelerator’ for low-latency decode.”
Wccftech
Inference is no longer monolithic. Reasoning and token
generation run on different architectures, each optimized for its
task. Dynamo orchestrates these systems, coordinating
compute, memory, and networking for maximum throughput.
Groq extends NVIDIA’s infrastructure stack, much like Mellanox
did for networking, adding a new pillar for low-latency decode.
Together, output rises and cost per token falls within the same
power envelope.

**Layout**

Two-zone layout on a full black slide: a product visual occupies the left ~58% (x 3.6 to 61.2) with its labels in two right-aligned and left-aligned columns beneath the chips, and a text column is set at x 64.4 to 96 on the right, with the quote at the top and the body paragraph below it. No title block, no footer, no slide number and no logo are visible; left and right margins are about 3.6% and 4%.

**Visuals**

One boxed photograph (not full-bleed, not cropped) at x 3.6 y 14.4 w 57.6 h 75.5 showing two chips on black. Left: a large square Rubin GPU package with a gold/bronze metal lid and frame, a black substrate and multiple gold silicon dies, with the central compute tiles in teal, purple and pink. Right: a smaller Groq 3 LPU package in the same gold frame, with a pink, orange and teal grid-patterned die, followed by seven progressively darker bronze cards trailing to the right, receding into the black. Lighting is soft and even from the front; the dominant colours are black, gold/bronze and the teal, pink and purple of the die. Also a green badge reading 55X (fill #76B900, black bold text) placed beside the 1,200 TB/s SRAM Bandwidth label, and two text labels for the chips. Specification figures are printed as text in two columns beneath the chips.

Background: Solid black #000000 covering the whole slide. Pure black background with white and light-grey type and a single green accent (#76B900). Title-style quote in NVIDIASans Medium (geometric sans, medium weight) about 4.6% of slide height in white #FFFFFF, with green #76B900 quotation marks. Attribution in NVIDIASans Regular, about 2.6% height, grey #A6A6A6. Body paragraph in NVIDIASans Regular, about 2.3% height, grey #A1A1A1, with generous line spacing (about 3.4% of slide height between baselines). Chip spec labels in small NVIDIASans Regular text, about 2.3% height, white-grey. Shapes are square-cornered; the green 55X badge is a flat rectangle with no shadow, gradient or border. No shadows or gradients anywhere.

### Slide 20

**Text**

Inference Inflection Arrives / Star History / GitHub Stars / 200K / 150K / 100K / 50K / 2012 / 2014 / 2016 / 2018 / 2020 / 2022 / 2024 / 2026 / Date / star-history.com / openclaw/openclaw / facebook/react / torvalds/linux / “NVIDIA goes all-in on agents at GTC / with toolkits, OpenClaw and models.” / SDxCentral / AI is moving beyond chatbots. It standardizes / how agents are built, orchestrated, and deployed at scale—marking the shift from / prompt-driven AI to autonomous software.

**Layout**

Two zones on a black slide: a left visual panel occupying about the left 59.5% (a chart card with a 3D mascot and title above it, centred around x 30%) and a right text column starting at about x 62% with a quote headline, attribution and two body paragraphs left-aligned on a common left edge. Headline quote sits at about y 33%, attribution at y 44%, body paragraphs at y 57% to 79%. No title bar, footer, page number or logo in the page furniture; the chart card's own watermark sits at its bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Chart: white rounded-rectangle card containing a line chart titled 'Star History' (hand-drawn style monospace title). Y-axis labelled 'GitHub Stars' with tick labels 50K, 100K, 150K, 200K (no units beyond K; range about 0 to 250K). X-axis labelled 'Date' with ticks 2012, 2014, 2016, 2018, 2020, 2022, 2024, 2026 (range 2012 to 2026). Three series: red line openclaw/openclaw, yellow line facebook/react, blue line torvalds/linux. No values are printed; about 2012 all lines near 0; blue about 5K at 2014 and about 200K near 2026; yellow about 170K at 2026; red near 0 until 2025 then rising almost vertically to about 240K at 2026. Blue and yellow are near-linear to convex, blue highest from about 2015 onward. Legend box top-left of plot area with black border, red square and red octopus-like icon for openclaw/openclaw, yellow square and blue infinity-like icon for facebook/react, blue square and Linux penguin-like icon for torvalds/linux. Highlighted point: none. Watermark: green star-like logo with 'star-history.com' bottom-right of card. Mascot: a glossy 3D-rendered red-orange lobster with big cartoon eyes and raised antennae, standing bottom-left, overlapping the lower-left corner of the chart card and casting a dark reflection below. The chart card has a faint mirrored reflection beneath it on the black floor. Lighting is soft studio light on the lobster; the image is full-bleed black at the left with a faint blue glow at the bottom right of the panel.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 with a dark gradient and faint blue glow in the lower-left and lower-right of the left panel. Black background (#000000) throughout with a dark charcoal gradient fading to black on the left panel. Headline quote in white #FFFFFF, NVIDIASans Medium, about 4.6% of slide height, with green quotation marks #76B900 in NVIDIASans Regular. Attribution 'SDxCentral' in grey #A6A6A6 NVIDIASans Regular at about 2.6% height. Body text in grey #A1A1A1 NVIDIASans Regular, about 2.3% of slide height, line spacing about 3.4% of slide height between lines, with a blank gap between paragraphs. Chart card is white #FFFFFF with rounded corners and no visible border; chart title and axis text in black #000000 monospace hand-drawn style; accent colours red #E8462E-like, yellow #F5E050-like and blue #3DA9E8-like for the series. Mascot is orange-red #D9472B-like with highlights. No shadows on text.

Two-column composition on a black field: a left text column starting at about 6 percent from the left edge holds a large pull quote, its attribution and three body paragraphs, all left-aligned; the right column holds a wide hand-drawn diagram occupying roughly x 39 to 98 and y 7 to 93. No title placeholder, footer or slide number is visible; the quote acts as the headline and the diagram carries its own heading at the top.

One illustration: a white-line hand-drawn infographic on black titled Announcing NVIDIA NemoClaw Reference OpenClaw with subtitle NVIDIA Agent Toolkit for Building Specialized Agents. Centre: a dashed circle with a caricature of a grey-haired man in a black jacket holding a red lobster in each hand, labelled NEMOCLAW. Right green-glowing rounded box (about x 88 to 98 percent, y 41 to 81 percent) with white outline containing a green hexagonal NVIDIA-style node icon labelled NEMOTRON NEMO DYNAMO NIM in green, a blue-and-grey stacked AI-Q block illustration labelled AI-Q in green, and a tan wireframe terrain surface labelled cuOPT in green. Dominant colours: black, white line art, bright green #76B900 glow and labels, purple, blue and tan illustration accents.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 across the full slide. Pure black background with white and light-grey type and a single bright NVIDIA green accent #76B900. Body paragraphs in regular sans-serif #A1A1A1, about 2.3 percent of slide height, with generous line spacing and gaps between paragraphs. ### Slide 22

**Text**

NVIDIA Leaderboard Topping Open Models / NVIDIA is World's Largest Contributor to Open-Source AI / 400 / 300 / Repos 200 / 100 / NVIDIA / Alibaba Cloud / Hugging Face / AI2 / Meta / Google / IBM / ByteDance / Baidu / Tencent / Microsoft / Zai / Jan 2025 / Feb 2026 / Source: Hugging Face / BioNeMo / Biology AI / Earth-2 / AI Physics / Nemotron / Agentic AI / Cosmos / Physical AI / GR00T / Robotics / Alpamayo / Autonomous Vehicles / “ / Open ecosystems drive hardware / adoption. What's new is they now / extend beyond compute into models, / agents, and orchestration. / ” / Bain & Company / Open frontier models and OpenClaw have moved AI from generation into / execution. Models reason, agents take action, and connect to tools, / memory, and workflows at scale. NVIDIA's open model ecosystem / enables developers and enterprises to build and deploy agentic systems / across industries.

**Layout**

Two-zone black slide. The left zone holds a centred title above a horizontal row of eight dark rounded cards fanned in perspective, with the large central chart card in front; the reflected card row fades into the bottom edge. The right zone is a left-aligned text column starting at x 65.1% with the quote at top, attribution beneath, and body paragraph lower down, all on a left margin of about 65% and a right margin of about 7%. No footer, page number, or logo sits in a corner of the slide.

**Visuals**

Illustration/diagram of eight cards arranged in a shallow arc with the centre one enlarged. Left to right: BioNeMo (Biology AI) showing a green-teal molecular chain on a dark card; Earth-2 (AI Physics) showing a gold-outlined globe with green land and blue ocean; Nemotron (Agentic AI) showing a gold line-drawn cartoon character waving; central chart card; Cosmos (Physical AI) showing a purple spiral galaxy with gold rim; GR00T (Robotics) showing a gold-outlined red humanoid robot waving; Alpamayo (Autonomous Vehicles) showing a gold-outlined yellow car. Each card is a dark charcoal rounded rectangle with a thin gold line-art style illustration and a white label beneath. The chart card is a near-black rounded panel with a thin grey border, no shadow visible, containing a line chart titled NVIDIA is World's Largest Contributor to Open-Source AI. Y-axis labelled Repos, ticks 100, 200, 300, 400, no unit beyond repos count, range 0 to about 420. X-axis from Jan 2025 to Feb 2026, unlabelled ticks. Series, each a cumulative line rising from near 0 at Jan 2025: NVIDIA in lime green #76B900 rising steepest to about 400 at Feb 2026 and labelled by a green NVIDIA logo badge at the top right of the line; Alibaba Cloud in orange-red line ending about 350 at Feb 2026, labelled with orange logo badge; Hugging Face and AI2 in yellow line stepping to about 230 at Feb 2026; Meta (blue), Google (green), IBM, ByteDance, Baidu, Tencent, Microsoft, Zai (grey) lines ending between about 60 and 150 at Feb 2026, each with a small coloured logo badge in a legend at the right. Source line Source: Hugging Face at bottom left in small grey text with the words Hugging Face underlined in blue; AI World logo badge (blue square with white mark and small AI WORLD text) at bottom right. Reflections of the cards and glowing blue and orange light spills appear on the black floor below the cards. A narrow vertical strip image at the right edge of the cards shows dim blue and orange glow.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 full-bleed, with the card illustration and reflections placed over it. Pure black background #000000 throughout. Title text off-white #F2F2F2 in NVIDIASans Medium (a geometric grotesque sans-serif), regular weight, normal case. Quote text white #FFFFFF in NVIDIASans Medium, about 4.6% of slide height per line, with line spacing about 4.9% of slide height. Opening and closing quotation marks in NVIDIA green #76B900. Attribution in NVIDIASans Regular #A6A6A6 about 2.6% of height. Body paragraph in NVIDIASans Regular #A1A1A1 about 2.3% of height with line spacing about 3.4% of height. Accent colour NVIDIA green #76B900 used for quote marks and the leaderboard lime line. Card shapes are dark charcoal rounded rectangles with soft corner radius and slight grey border; no gradients on text. Chart panel has thin mid-grey outline.

**Layout**

Two-zone layout on a black field. The title sits top-left at about x 12.2%, y 18.9%, left-aligned and well above the map. The 3D world map occupies the lower-left, spanning roughly x 1.3% to 63% and y 27.7% to 100%, with its edges fading into the black background. **Visuals**

One 3D-rendered world map (about 2939 by 1324 px source) seen in a slight perspective tilt, with extruded landmass edges. City lights glow warm gold-amber (about #F2C27A) on dark brown-black (about #2A2118) land, with the dimmest regions near black; the map is lit only by its own city lights against a pure black background, and it is not cropped, boxed or tinted. Overlaid on it are about 17 map pins, each a round white-rimmed flag marker (USA, Spain, Germany, Poland, France, Israel, Saudi Arabia, UAE, India, South Korea, Japan, Vietnam, Singapore, Indonesia, Brazil and others) on a short stem pointing to the location, each paired with a black rounded label tag with white sans-serif text. Small gold coin-stack database icons sit beside the labels for Linagora, United States, Institute of Science Tokyo Stockmark, Gnani.ai, AI Singapore and WideLabs. The map is not a chart: no axes, legend or data series.

Background: Solid black #000000, with the 3D night-lit world map placed on it.

### Slide 24

**Text**

“A turnkey solution to help plan, / build and maintain AI factories.” / Forbes / DSX defines how AI factories are built as a system. It integrates / compute, networking, storage, power, and cooling into a single / architecture—from rack to pod to gigawatt scale. With simulation / and co-design, factories are optimized before deployment, / maximizing tokens per watt and ensuring the highest throughput / and lowest cost per token. / NVIDIA DSX AI Factory Platform / Extreme Co-Design at Infrastructure Scale / Rubin DSX / Reference Designs / DSX / Ecosystem / Omniverse / DSX Blueprint / Reference Designs, / Best Practices, / Methodologies / DSX Max-Q / DSX Flex / DSX Exchange / DSX Sim / DSX Libraries, / APIs, Software / Power / Liquid Cooling / Chips, Systems, / Facilities

**Layout**

Two-column split: a left text column (roughly x 6 to 34) holds a large quotation with attribution above a body paragraph, vertically centred in the lower-middle of the slide; the right half (roughly x 36 to 100) holds a dark diagram. No visible title placeholder, footer, page number or logo; margins are about 6% on the left and the text is left-aligned.

**Visuals**

One diagram, a dark rendered illustration titled NVIDIA DSX AI Factory Platform with subtitle Extreme Co-Design at Infrastructure Scale. Top row, a horizontal line connecting three square tiles labelled Rubin DSX Reference Designs (grey server racks photo), DSX Ecosystem (dark tile with small coloured logo grid), and Omniverse DSX Blueprint (render of a building interior); the row is labelled at left as Reference Designs, Best Practices, Methodologies. Middle row, a horizontal line connecting four tiles labelled DSX Max-Q (dark tile with rows of coloured cylinders, red, orange and yellow), DSX Flex (grey server rack photo), DSX Exchange (dark tilted cabinets photo), and DSX Sim (white-grey data hall photo); the row is labelled at left as DSX Libraries, APIs, Software. Bottom row, labelled at left as Chips, Systems, Facilities, a line runs to a small tile labelled Power (gold cylindrical components on black) and then to a long horizontal strip of many gold-and-black server racks, which connects to a small tile labelled Liquid Cooling (dark tile with pipes). Two thin diagonal leader lines run from the Power tile and the Liquid Cooling tile down to a large isometric illustration at the bottom: a grey-black multi-level data centre building with a raised central block, rows of glowing warm-white light strips along the roofs, and a dark blue-grey glow at the lower right. The building sits on a black ground and is cropped at the bottom edge of the slide. Lighting is dark with warm white highlights; dominant colours are black, charcoal grey, gold and warm white with a faint blue glow at lower right. Composition is centred on the diagram with the building as the base; the image is full-height within the right half, not boxed.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 full-bleed, with the diagram image sitting on it and a dark fade on its left edge. Pure black background with white and grey type; green accent used only on the quotation marks. Quote and title text in NVIDIA Sans Medium (a geometric sans-serif) white #FFFFFF, about 4.6% of slide height for the quote lines. Attribution Forbes in NVIDIA Sans Regular #A6A6A6, about 2.6%. Body paragraph in NVIDIA Sans Regular grey #A1A1A1, about 2.3% of slide height with generous line spacing (about 3.4% line pitch). Opening and closing quotation marks in green #76B900. Diagram labels are small white and light-grey sans-serif, with row labels in khaki-grey #A8A07A; lines are thin grey hairlines about 0.1% of slide height; tiles are square with small rounded corners and light grey outlines. A soft dark gradient fades the left edge of the diagram into the black background.

### Slide 25

**Text**

“NVIDIA expands robotics ecosystem at GTC as physical AI moves toward large-scale deployment.”
TrendForce
Physical AI is the next wave of AI. It is moving from simulation into the real world. Robotics, industrial automation, and autonomous systems are scaling across
manufacturing, logistics, and mobility. NVIDIA works with virtually every major robotics developer. Its full-stack platform spans training, simulation, and
deployment, enabling a global ecosystem where robots become a new class of workforce.

**Layout**

Single centred column across the upper quarter of the slide: a one-line headline with quote marks, a small grey attribution line beneath it, and a three-line centred paragraph. The headline row sits about 11 percent from the top, the paragraph ends near 34 percent of the height, and the lower two-thirds of the slide is a full-width photographic band running edge to edge with no margins. No footer, logo or page number is visible.

**Visuals**

One photographic render, full-bleed across the whole width and bottom-aligned, covering roughly the lower 73 percent of the slide. It shows an indoor exhibition hall with a black angular truss ceiling forming a large triangle, a dark grey back wall carrying the NVIDIA logo, and a tiered stage holding rows of humanoid robots. A yellow Caterpillar excavator stands at left with a CAT logo on its cab, and yellow and orange industrial robot arms stand at right. Two white-and-grey robot arms and several small wheeled robots sit in the foreground, and three cars (a silver sedan at about centre right, a grey-green coupe, and a blue sports car) are parked at the lower right edge. Some humanoid robots stand on balconies at upper left and upper right. The composition is wide and centred on the stage. Lighting is soft, low-key overhead light with a dim, moody look. Dominant colours are black, charcoal grey, with yellow, silver and blue accents. The top of the render fades into black through a dark gradient strip, so the image has no hard upper edge. Nothing is tinted or overlaid apart from that fade.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 fill covering the slide, with the photographic render band placed over its lower part. Pure black background (#000000) across the whole slide. The headline is white (#FFFFFF) NVIDIASans Medium, sans-serif, about 4.6 percent of slide height. The opening and closing quotation marks are NVIDIA green (#76B900) NVIDIASans Regular at about 5.2 percent. The attribution is light grey (#A6A6A6) NVIDIASans Regular, about 2.6 percent. The body paragraph is mid grey (#A1A1A1) NVIDIASans Regular, about 2.3 percent, with generous line spacing. Shapes: none besides the full-slide black fill and the photo fade. No corner radii, shadows or borders.

### Slide 26

**Text**

“Uber, NVIDIA plan robotaxi rollout / in 28 cities starting next year.”
Reuters
Uber and NVIDIA are scaling autonomous mobility worldwide. / Powered by NVIDIA DRIVE, robotaxi fleets are rolling out across 28 / cities, bringing autonomous transportation into everyday use. This / marks the transition from pilot programs to global deployment, as / autonomous systems move from demonstration into commercial / infrastructure at scale.

**Layout**

Full-bleed black slide with a text column on the left (quote headline, source line, then body paragraph) and a photograph spanning the centre and right. The quote sits at about 33% from the top, left-aligned at about 7% from the left margin; the body paragraph starts at about 57.6% down and is left-aligned. No title placeholder, footer, or slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Photograph (centre, about 31% to 100% horizontally, 10% to 100% vertically): a dark-grey transparent-bodied sedan rendered in 3D, seen from the front three-quarter angle, with glowing cyan-blue wiring harness lines, sensor and computer modules highlighted in cyan boxes along the wheel arches, sills, bumper and roof, and a reflective black floor beneath it. Lighting is dramatic low-key with cool white highlights on the bodywork and bonnet edge. Dominant colours are black, charcoal grey and cyan #3FC8E8. The car is not cropped; the image blends into the black background with a dark gradient at its edges. Second photograph (right, about 30% to 41% horizontally, full height): a close-up of a black printed circuit board with two large square processor chips with green-and-orange heatsink patterns, multiple connectors along the bottom edge with cyan-blue ports, reflected on a dark glossy surface. The board is not cropped and fades into black at its lower edge.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 with a faint dark gradient behind the photographs. Black background (#000000) throughout with no gradient on the flat fill. Quote and body text are white #FFFFFF for the headline and grey #A1A1A1 for body, with source line #A6A6A6. Accent colour is NVIDIA green #76B900 for the quote marks. Typeface is NVIDIA Sans, a geometric humanist sans-serif; headline Medium weight, body and source Regular. Headline size about 4.6% of slide height (cap height about 3.2%), body about 2.3% of slide height, source about 2.6%. ### Slide 27

**Text**

“NVIDIA betting big on ‘physical AI’.” / Punchbowl News / Physical AI is moving from simulation into the real world. In partnership with / Disney, NVIDIA brought Olaf to life using physics-based simulation, foundation / models, and real-time robotics. At the same time, automakers and mobility / leaders are standardizing on NVIDIA’s AV platform to scale autonomous / systems globally. Physical AI is no longer experimental. It is entering / production across industries.

**Layout**

Two-column split with no visible grid. The left column is a text block occupying roughly the left 42% of the slide, vertically centred, with its left edge at about 6.2% of the width. The quote headline sits at about 38% from the top, followed by the attribution line and a gap, then a six-line body paragraph starting at about 58% from the top and ending near 77%. The right 58% is a full-height photograph bleeding off the top, right and bottom edges. No title placeholder, footer, slide number, date or logo is present.

**Visuals**

One photograph, full-bleed on the right of the slide from x 41.7% to the right edge and full height. Subject: a large plush Olaf snowman (cream-white body, red carrot nose, twig arms and hair) sitting on a wooden floor in front of a huge projected close-up of Olaf's face on a bright screen. A man with grey hair and glasses, wearing a black leather jacket and black trousers, sits on the floor beside the plush, leaning back on one hand and looking up toward the projection. Composition: the plush is in the left-centre of the frame, the man at right, the projected face fills the upper half and background. Lighting: warm, strong spotlight from the screen, with dark shadowed foreground. Dominant colours: black, cream white, peach and orange, red nose, dark navy at top right. The photograph is cropped at the top, bottom and right edges and is not boxed. A second narrow strip of the same image (about 14.7% wide, at x 40.9%) sits over the left edge of the photo and fades into the black left panel, acting as a soft gradient-like transition with no visible border.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 fill covering the whole slide, with the photograph on the right half. Black background throughout with a flat, minimal, dark editorial look. Headline quote in white (#FFFFFF) with NVIDIA green (#76B900) opening and closing quotation marks. Attribution line in mid grey (#A6A6A6). Body paragraph in light grey (#A1A1A1). Typeface is NVIDIA Sans, a geometric humanist sans-serif: Medium weight for the headline and Regular for the attribution and body. Headline is about 4.6% of slide height, attribution about 2.6%, body about 2.3%. Body is set in left-aligned ragged-right lines with generous line spacing of about 3.4% of slide height per line. No shapes, rules, corner radii, shadows or borders. The photograph has no frame.

### Slide 29

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Groups analyst quotes by theme on a single slide, letting third-party voices carry the case without extra text.
- What would change the score: Condense the photo montages and the quote walls (slides 29 and 30) into a few purposeful pages so the argument from slides 7 to 28 reaches the close without interruption.

**Text**

What the analysts said: / “NVIDIA-based inference has clear cost per token leadership that gets better with Rubin.” / Morgan Stanley / “Continued leadership backed by broadening full-stack end-to-end pipeline, extreme co-design with customers, and supply assurance.” / BofA / “Strong growth outlook for 2027, consistent with our estimates and well above the Street.” / Goldman Sachs / “The $1T+ in Blackwell and Vera Rubin purchase orders/demand through CY27 is explicitly a floor.” / JPMorgan / “We came out of the keynote reassured in NVIDIA’s roadmap and continued ability to innovate faster than the competition.” / Citi / “Super Bowl atmosphere, impromptu concert, and technical breakthroughs … GTC at its finest.” / Cantor / “Frankly we increasingly wonder how anyone else can compete with this.” / Bernstein / “NVIDIA is the clear end-to-end infrastructure partner with the enabling software stack.” / Melius

**Layout**

Three-column by three-row grid of equal rounded quote cards in the lower two-thirds and centre, with the title ‘What the analysts said:’ placed top-left in the empty left column aligned to the middle card row's upper area. The grid is inset from the left and right edges with roughly 2.7% margins, the cards are separated by gaps of about 6.8% of slide width horizontally and 8.3% vertically, and the top card row starts at about 8.3% from the top. Quote text is centred in each card with the attribution centred beneath it. No footer, slide number or logo is visible.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed photograph of a large arena or conference hall filling with a dense blue-lit audience, shot from an elevated wide angle with a central perspective receding into the distance; crowd and stage lights glow blue and violet with warm orange light points on the seating rows. The photo is cropped to the full slide, darkened by a black overlay (about 55 to 60 percent opacity) so the cards and text stand out. Each of the nine quote cards is a dark translucent rounded rectangle with a thin blue-violet border; no charts, tables, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background: Black #000000 base with a full-bleed darkened photograph of a blue-lit arena crowd over it. Dark, cinematic look: a near-black base with a darkened blue arena photograph behind everything. Title text is white #FFFFFF in NVIDIASans Medium (a geometric grotesque sans-serif, medium weight) at about 5.2 percent of slide height, sentence case. Quote body text is white #FFFFFF in NVIDIASans Regular (sans-serif, regular weight) at about 2.6 percent of slide height, centred, with about 1.3 line spacing. Opening and closing quotation marks are accent green #76B900 in NVIDIASans Medium. Attributions are grey #A6A6A6 in NVIDIASans Medium at about 2.3 percent of slide height, centred. Cards are dark navy #0A0F2A at roughly 60 percent opacity with a 1 px border in muted blue #2A3C8A, corner radius about 1 percent of slide width, and no visible shadow.

### Slide 30

**Review notes**

- What would change the score: Condense the photo montages and the quote walls (slides 29 and 30) into a few purposeful pages so the argument from slides 7 to 28 reaches the close without interruption.

**Text**

What the press said:
“NVIDIA is sustaining its leadership
in the AI chip market.”
Reuters
“NVIDIA is the engine powering
the AI revolution.”
Lex Fridman Podcast
“Vera Rubin offers a stunning 40,000,000x
compute growth within a decade.”
Wccftech
“$1 trillion won’t be enough
to meet AI demand.”
Fortune
“NVIDIA remains at the forefront of the AI
demand curve for 2026 and beyond.”
Handelsblatt
“Every industry is here. Every tech company is here.
Every AI company is here.”
All-In Podcast
“NVIDIA is the default platform where
businesses build serious AI systems.”
The Street
“No other event has brought together as
many industries, companies, AI influencers,
VCs, and startups in one place.”
eWEEK

**Layout**

A three-column by three-row grid of rounded quote cards fills the right and centre of the slide, with the top-left cell left empty and holding the title 'What the press said:' left-aligned at the same height as the top row of cards. Cards sit in three columns at about x 1.1%, 34.8% and 68.6% and three rows at about y 4.6%, 35.9% and 66.3%, with cards about 30% of slide width and 29% of slide height; there is no header, footer, logo or slide number.

**Visuals**

Background photograph: a wide, full-bleed, heavily blurred (shallow depth of field) photograph of a dense crowd of conference attendees on a trade-show floor, with exhibition booths, screens and lanyards visible as soft shapes. The composition is a crowd seen from slightly above and in front, filling the whole frame, with bright white and warm highlights at the lower-left and right edges and darker areas in the centre and top. It is cropped to the full slide, with a dark overlay (black at about 55 to 65% opacity) darkening the top and centre. Dominant colours: near-black, charcoal grey, muted blue-grey and small warm white highlights. Card panels: nine text cards are not present; eight translucent rounded rectangles hold the quotes, each a dark grey fill with a thin light-grey outline; no icons, logos or charts.

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed blurred photograph of a crowd on a trade-show floor under a black overlay (#000000 at about 60% opacity). Black background with a blurred dark crowd photograph and a black overlay; title and quote text in white #FFFFFF, quote marks and accents in NVIDIA green #76B900, attribution names in mid grey #A6A6A6. Typeface is NVIDIASans, a humanist geometric sans-serif: Medium for the title and quote marks and attributions, Regular for quote text. Title is about 5.24% of slide height, quote body about 2.61%, attribution about 2.29%. Cards are translucent dark grey (#1E1E1E at about 45% opacity) with 1px light grey (#8A8A8A) strokes and rounded corners of about 1.2% of slide width; no shadows or gradients on the cards.

### Slide 31

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Presents event scale as five large numbers with small labels, a clean use of a stat row.
- Review: The deck has a strong, consistent identity: black ground, green accent, large quoted headlines, and polished photography and renders (slides 1, 13, 31).
- Worth keeping: Keep the dark, green-accent system, the large quote headlines with attribution, the clean cost-per-token chart on slide 13 with its labeled 2X, 3X and 10X callouts, and the stat strip on slide 31.

**Text**

436
 Exhibitors and
Sponsors
45K
Virtual
Attendees
39M
Keynote
Views
35K
In-Person
Attendees
850+
Talks and
Sessions

**Layout**

A single row of five centred statistic groups sits across the upper part of the slide, roughly from 20% to 80% of the width, with each number above a two-line label. The photograph fills the whole slide behind them, so there is no title, footer or slide number, and the stats sit over open sky at the top.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed aerial photograph of the NVIDIA GTC conference centre in San Jose, shot from above and to the side in bright daylight under a clear pale-blue sky. Foreground at left shows a tall glass office tower edge and a flat grey rooftop with rooftop equipment. Centre shows a long, low curved silver-grey metal roof and a tall blue-and-tan office tower behind it, with distant brown hills and mountains on the horizon. A green panel with the NVIDIA GTC logo and the words 'It All Starts Here' is set on the glass entrance, with a US flag, a California state flag and a third white flag on poles in front. Right side shows a grey multi-storey building and a white angular roof. Lower right shows people on a plaza, white umbrellas and a small NVIDIA GTC sign. A white open metal sculpture stands in the plaza centre. The photo is cropped at all four edges. Dominant colours are sky blue, silver-grey, lime green and tan. Five translucent soft-edged image glows (about 20.6% tall) sit behind the statistic numbers, apparently as soft halos.

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed photograph of the NVIDIA GTC venue over a #000000 fill. Black #000000 background behind a full-bleed photograph. Numbers are white #FFFFFF in NVIDIASans Medium, a sans-serif with a faint drop shadow; numbers are about 8.8% of slide height. Labels are black #000000 in NVIDIASans Bold, a sans-serif, about 2% of slide height, in sentence case. No accent colour appears in the text. No lines, rounded corners or gradients are visible, other than the soft halo images behind the numbers.

### Slide 32

**Review notes**

- Review: Slides 32 to 43 are photo grids with no message, and slides 45 to 72 repeat earlier content as two-up thumbnails with text too small to read, some with near-empty chart frames (slide 57).

**Text**

ALL-IN

**Layout**

Four-panel photo mosaic on a full-bleed black slide with no title, footer, or slide number. A tall panel fills the left half, and the right half holds two equal squares side by side across the top and one wide panel across the bottom, with thin black gutters between them.

**Visuals**

Four photographs. (1) Left, 0 to 49.6% wide and full height: five men seated behind a black desk on a stage. From left, a man in a black t-shirt, a man in a purple check jacket, a man in a navy jacket, a man with grey hair and glasses in a black leather jacket over a black t-shirt at centre, and a man in a grey blazer on the right. Red eyeglasses lie on the desk at far left, and audience members are visible at the bottom right of the seating. The desk front is black with large white bold sans-serif capitals reading ALL-IN, running across the lower right. Background: a dark stage with angled linear light fixtures at top left, a green vertical panel, an orange railing, and a large screen showing people at top centre. The photo is cropped to the frame, not boxed. (2) Top-middle, 50.5 to 74.8% wide and top 48.9% high: a television camera on a tripod in the foreground with a monitor showing the scene, pointed at a seated panel in a blurred trade-show hall with blue-lit booths and rectangular ceiling lights. A second camera is visible at right. Dominant colours: black, with blue and white highlights. (3) Top-right, 75.7 to 100% wide and top 48.9% high: a close-up, eye-level portrait of two men standing side by side. The left man has grey hair, rectangular glasses, and a black leather jacket over a black t-shirt. The right man has short dark hair, a dark suit, white shirt, and dark tie. His arm rests on the other man's shoulder. The background is blurred green and grey, and the framing is waist-up. Soft even lighting. (4) Bottom-right, 50.5 to 100% wide and 51.1 to 100% down: an interview set in an exhibition hall with a grey floor, a backlit display case with illuminated panels at centre, and a dark wall. A man in a navy suit and purple tie sits on a stool at left, gesturing with one hand and holding papers on his knee. A man in a black leather jacket sits in profile at right, facing him. Camera rigs and lighting stands are at the left edge, and a camera is partly visible at the far right. Cool, neutral lighting with dark tones.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 fill covering the full slide. Full-bleed black background with no text styling beyond the ALL-IN wordmark, which is white bold sans-serif capitals (Helvetica or Univers Bold style, about 35% of slide height), tracked normally, and aligned right inside the left photo. Accent green #76B900 appears only as a rectangle behind the top-right photo and in the green panel within the left photo. Photos sit edge to edge with about 0.5% black gutters, with square corners, no shadows, and no gradients.

### Slide 33

**Review notes**

- Review: Slides 32 to 43 are photo grids with no message, and slides 45 to 72 repeat earlier content as two-up thumbnails with text too small to read, some with near-empty chart frames (slide 57).

**Text**

“Every industrial company will / become a robotics company.” / Manufacturing Dive / “Factories themselves are now robotic systems. If you take the / combination of all of those, you can combine them all together / to create a system, a factory that’s essentially a robot itself.” / Rev Lebaredian, NVIDIA / AI Factories Turn Compute into Revenue / Design / Simulate / Build / Operate / NVIDIA GTC / NVIDIA GTC

**Layout**

Two-zone composition: a full-height stage photograph occupies the left ~63% of the slide and a second, narrower photograph sits in the centre band, while the quote block is right-aligned in the black area at the right, starting about 64% from the left and vertically centred around the middle of the slide. No title bar, no footer, no slide number; the quote text is left-aligned in a single column with generous black margin on the right.

**Visuals**

Photograph 1 (left, full height): a wide shot of a large conference stage with a speaker in a dark suit standing centre-stage, gesturing with his right hand, facing the audience. A green NVIDIA GTC podium sits in the foreground left of him, and two tall green NVIDIA GTC banners stand at the left edge and on the right of the stage. Behind him a large rear projection screen shows a slide titled 'AI Factories Turn Compute into Revenue' with four panels labelled Design, Simulate, Build, Operate, each showing a rendered image (a server rack, a blue wireframe dome, a building model, and a factory floor plan); below the panels is a row of partner logos and a blue-lit server-floor graphic. Foreground audience silhouettes fill the bottom of the frame. Lighting is dim with stage spotlight on the presenter and screen glow; dominant colours black, grey, lime green #76B900 and blue. The photo is cropped at the left edge and blends into black at its right edge, with a dark gradient fade toward the right. Photograph 2 (centre band, full height): a tall vertical image of a dark grey robot arm and a humanoid figure silhouette at the left, against black, with an industrial robotic arm in the right portion; it is a cropped, full-height image with no border. Both photographs are unboxed and unrounded.

**Design**

Background pure black #000000 throughout. Headline quote in NVIDIASans Medium, white #FFFFFF, roughly 4.6% of slide height per line, with the opening and closing quotation marks in NVIDIASans Regular lime green #76B900. Attribution 'Manufacturing Dive' and body quote in NVIDIASans Regular, grey #A6A6A6 and #A1A1A1 at about 2.3% of slide height; speaker credit in the same grey style. Sans-serif geometric type, regular and medium weights, no italics. Accent colour lime green #76B900. No shapes, rules or shadows; the only shape-like elements are the green banners and podium in the photographs.

**Layout**

Full-height photograph bleeds off the left edge across about the left half of the slide, fading into black on its right side. One wide photograph of a speaker on a stage at NVIDIA GTC, placed as a full-height left-bleed image (2257 by 1699 px). The subject is a presenter in a dark blazer and light trousers standing centre-stage, gesturing with one hand, with a large lit projection screen behind him at left showing the heading 'with NVIDIA Classical AV Stack', a street-view dashboard video (a road with a cyclist and cars), and a small text panel. A green NVIDIA GTC podium stands at left foreground, and a tall green vertical banner with the NVIDIA GTC logo stands behind the presenter. Audience members are silhouetted in the lower foreground in a dark theatre with rows of seats and a warm brown stage front. Lighting is a bright spotlight on the stage floor against a black surround, so the image is dark overall. Dominant colours are black, grey stage floor, NVIDIA green #76B900 and warm brown #5A2E1A. The photo is not boxed, has no tint, and fades to black at the right edge. A second overlapping image of the same stage (973 by 1696 px) is placed at x 30.4% and fills the centre of the first; it shows the green NVIDIA GTC banner with its white logo and the presenter in the middle. No other graphics appear.

Background: Solid black #000000 across the whole slide, with a full-height photograph of a stage on the left half. Pure black background with a full-bleed photograph on the left half. The attribution 'TechRadar' is grey #A6A6A6 NVIDIASans Regular at about 2.6% of height. The body quotation and the speaker line are grey #A1A1A1 NVIDIASans Regular at about 2.3% of height, with line spacing of about 3.4% of height between body lines. NVIDIA GTC / SPX Switches / SPX SuperNICs / NVIDIA GTC / NAPA

**Layout**

Full-bleed photo collage of six photographs on a black background with thin black gutters, arranged in two rows and roughly three columns. No title, footer, or slide number is shown.

**Visuals**

Six photographs. (1) Top-left: a rear view of an audience in a conference hall facing a stage with a green NVIDIA GTC screen and a presenter in a dark shirt; audience members wear lanyards and one shirt reads NAPA; the screen to its right shows a diagram of four blue GPU boxes labelled GPU, each linked by lines to a row labelled SPX SuperNICs, with a top label SPX Switches. (2) Top-centre: two seated speakers in white armchairs in a dark venue, a man in a beige sweater at left and a woman in a black top and beige trousers at right gesturing with both hands; a water bottle sits between them. (3) Top-right: a wide stage shot with a large white robotic arm holding a purple and grey part above a black grid table, four seated panellists in white armchairs, and a presenter in a navy jacket gesturing at right in front of a bright green wall with white NVIDIA GTC logo. (4) Bottom-left: a wide shot of six panellists seated in white armchairs on a stage under a blue faceted ceiling with lights, a glass-walled facility behind; the speakers wear dark jacket, light grey sweater, black sweater, beige jacket, lavender sweater and khaki jacket. (5) Bottom-centre-right: three seated people in white armchairs against a dark background: an older man in a blue checked shirt with arms crossed, a woman in a blue blazer, and a woman with long hair in a dark jacket, with water bottles on a table. (6) Bottom-right: two men in suits gesturing and laughing on a dark red-curtained stage, one with a bald head and the other with glasses and a grey suit. Lighting is stage lighting with warm highlights; dominant colours are green, blue, black and white. Photos are rectangular, unrounded, not cropped to shapes, with no overlays or tints.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 covering the whole slide. Pure black #000000 background behind the collage with black gutters between photos. Photos are tiled edge to edge with no borders or captions. Green #76B900 rectangles sit behind the photos at the NVIDIA GTC green positions and are visible as framing colour. No typography is set as design text; the only type is the NVIDIA GTC wordmark in white on the green wall and the SPX labels in the diagram in a light sans-serif. No shadows, gradients, or rounded corners.

**Review notes**

A Gear Store (partial sign, left of photo 2) / NVIDIA (on cap crown, photo 7) / NVIDIA (on cap badge, photo 7) / Staff (on green T-shirt, photo 6) / GTC (on green T-shirt logo, photo 6) / T-Collection (sign, photo 8, partly legible)

**Layout**

A 4-column by 2-row grid of eight equal-sized photographs filling the slide edge to edge on a black field, with no title, footer, or slide number. Each tile is 24.4% of slide width and about 48.9% of slide height, with gutters of 0.8% horizontally and about 2.3% vertically. Columns start at x 0, 25.2, 50.4 and 75.6; rows start at y 0 and 51.2. Tiles are square-cornered and aligned on a common grid with a black margin of about 0.8% between them and at the outer edges.

**Visuals**

Eight photographs of an event merchandise store (the Gear Store) and its visitors, arranged as a grid. Photo 1 (top left, x 0, y 0): two men, one in black jacket with glasses on the left and one with glasses and grey hair on the right, both holding up a dark green knitted sweater with a knitted cartoon figure of a man in a black jacket; bright green wall and clothing racks behind, soft daylight, shallow depth of field. Photo 2 (x 25.2, y 0): elevated view of a large store interior with white counters, a sign reading A Gear Store on the left, black merchandise on shelves, many attendees browsing and a staffed checkout line, a bright green back wall, mezzanine railings above, neutral white and grey lighting. Photo 3 (x 50.4, y 0): woman in a mauve jacket and purple beanie handling a white plush toy on a counter covered in stacked neon-green lidded bowls, a man in a blue polo holding paper behind her, black jackets on racks, green wall. Photo 4 (x 75.6, y 0): smiling woman with curly blond hair in a navy bomber jacket holding a neon-green visor-style cap, black garments and a backpack at right, blurred figures behind. Photo 5 (x 0, y 51.2): close view of a hand with bangles reaching toward a stack of black mugs with neon-green rims and dog-silhouette prints on a wooden table, blurred background. Photo 6 (x 25.2, y 51.2): smiling man with long braided hair in a bright green NVIDIA GTC Staff T-shirt with a lanyard badge, a second man in a black polo with a lanyard standing beside him, green wall and grey pillar behind. **Review notes**

- Review: Slides 32 to 43 are photo grids with no message, and slides 45 to 72 repeat earlier content as two-up thumbnails with text too small to read, some with near-empty chart frames (slide 57).

**Text**

Claw Bar / We're Excited to Help You / Build-a-Claw / NVIDIA GTC / Ask / NVIDIA NemoClaw / Run Autonomous Agents More Safely / Watch Replay / Get Started / Build-a-Claw

**Layout**

Seven photographs form a borderless grid of two rows on a solid black field, with black gutters of about 1 percent of slide width between images and black margins at the left, right, top and bottom edges. The top row has four equal photos of 24.3 percent width and 48.9 percent height, starting at x 0, 25.2, 50.4 and 75.7 percent. The bottom row starts at y 51.1 percent and holds a narrow photo at x 0, a double-width photo spanning x 25.2 to 74.8 percent, and a narrow photo at x 75.7. There is no title, footer, page number or slide number.

**Visuals**

Seven full-bleed rectangular photographs, none cropped to a shape and none with an overlay or tint. Top row, left to right: (1) an outdoor event plaza under white canopy tents and white patio umbrellas, with white high bar tables, a black stanchion queue and a sign reading Claw Bar, and a sign reading We're Excited to Help You, with attendees walking; daylight, bright, white and grey tones with green foliage at top left. ### Slide 42

**Review notes**

- Review: Slides 32 to 43 are photo grids with no message, and slides 45 to 72 repeat earlier content as two-up thumbnails with text too small to read, some with near-empty chart frames (slide 57).

**Text**

Poster Gallery / NVIDIA GTC / GTC Park / Red Hat / NVIDIA / ORDER HERE / PICK UP HERE / Mid-3 and CSAL for Performance and Resilience (signage and poster text visible inside the photographs, in reading order)

**Layout**

A full-bleed mosaic of seven rectangular photographs arranged as a 4-column by 2-row grid with thin black gutters between cells. The bottom-middle photograph spans two columns. The slide has no title, no body text, no footer, no slide number and no margins; the photographs run to the slide edges. The green rectangle sits behind the bottom-right photograph.

**Visuals**

Seven event photographs of the NVIDIA GTC conference, all rectangular with square corners and no borders. Top row, left to right: (1) a daylight plaza with a green 'Poster Gallery' banner structure, a beige colonial-style building with a clock tower and a glass office tower at left, and a crowd of attendees with lanyards; (2) a woman in a mauve top and green lanyard pointing at a research poster board, with a man in a dark jacket beside her, the poster titled 'Mid-3 and CSAL for Performance and Resilience'; (3) a green GTC arch with the 'NVIDIA GTC' logo and a 'GTC Park' sign, flanked by lattice triangles, with attendees crossing a pedestrian crosswalk under trees and white tents behind; (4) a seated man in a grey sweater looking at his phone beside a man with a lanyard walking a black delivery robot bearing the NVIDIA logo, under string lights and trees. Bottom row, left to right: (5) a cornhole game on green turf at dusk with men in black shirts, a red and white 'Red Hat' banner with the NVIDIA logo, and string lights overhead; (6) a wide aerial dusk view of an illuminated plaza with a glowing green and white 'NVIDIA' sign at lower left, several black robots, a white marquee tent, a green food truck with solar panels, yellow lane stripes and string lights; (7) a dense crowd of attendees with lanyards in front of white tents with paper globe lights overhead, with 'ORDER HERE' and 'PICK UP HERE' signs on a tent. Lighting is mostly natural daylight in the top row and warm artificial dusk light in the bottom row. Dominant colours are green, white, grey and warm amber. No photograph is cropped to a shape and none has an overlay or tint.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000. Black #000000 background showing through thin gutters. No typography is set on the slide. The accent colour is NVIDIA green #76B900, visible only as a block behind the bottom-right photograph. Photographs are unbordered, with square corners and no shadows.

### Slide 44

**Text**

This is the next phase of computing.
AI is infrastructure. A five-layer stack from energy to applications. Inference has become thinking, and every interaction generates tokens. AI factories convert energy into tokens, producing intelligence at scale.
Throughput lowers cost per token and increases revenue. In the AI factory era, tokens per second per watt translate directly into revenue. Through extreme co-design, NVIDIA delivers the lowest cost per token and the highest revenue per watt.
NVIDIA builds the full platform—CUDA-X, DSX infrastructure, and Vera Rubin systems—connected to a global ecosystem.
Agentic AI is here. Physical AI is next. We are just at the beginning.
Signature (handwritten)

**Layout**

Black full-bleed slide with no title, no footer and no slide number. Text sits in two left-aligned columns at about x 10.5% and x 35.6%, starting near y 29% and ending near y 70%, with the heading line above the left column. A stage photograph of a presenter fills the right side from x 45.4% to the right edge, full height, and a white handwritten signature sits centred under the right text column at about x 40%, y 63%.

**Visuals**

One photograph: a grey-haired man with rimless glasses, wearing a black leather jacket and dark trousers, facing right and gesturing with both open hands while holding a small black clicker in his left hand. He is set against a near-black stage background with soft stage light on his face and jacket; the photo is not cropped at the top or bottom, is not boxed, and blends into the black slide. One handwritten white signature, a cursive name-style autograph, drawn in thin white strokes on black with no box or frame. No charts, tables, logos or icons. No overlay or tint is visible beyond the natural darkness of the photo.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 covering the whole slide. Pure black background (#000000) with no gradient or pattern. Body text is mid grey #A1A1A1 in NVIDIASans Regular, sans-serif, about 2.3% of slide height; the opening line is NVIDIASans Bold in the same grey. The signature and photograph are white or natural colour. No accent colour, no rules, no shapes, no corner radii, no shadows.

**Review notes**
