- Suggested repair: Raise body text to a readable size and contrast, cut the repeated two-up slides 45 to 72 or rebuild them as single full-size slides, and fix the empty chart frames on slide 57.

**Text**

NVIDIA GTC / 2026 / 3RD APPEARANCE / 2X / SARAH GUO / 2ND APPEARANCE / 2026 / HOST CITY / ALFRED LIN / 2ND APPEARANCE / 2026 / HOST CITY / GAVIN BAKER / HARRISON CHASE / JACOMO CORBO / JOE GREED / MICHAEL DELL / ANIRUDH DEVGAN / MARK DELSTONE / AIDAN GOMEZ / MICHAEL INTRATOR / AKI_JAIN / TIFFANY JANZEN / ARTHUR MENSCH / DANIEL MAGIER / DINESH KHIRWANI / DEEPAK PANKAR / LIN QIAO / SAM RODRIGUES / REIHN ROMBACH / AARAVIND SRINIVAS / PETER STEINBERGER / RAOUL WEISMAN / VINCENT BRUSSEL

**Layout**

Two equal boxed panels side by side on a black slide, each with a thin rounded border, the left panel at x 4.8% and the right at x 51.3%, both at y 18.1% with width 43.9% and height 63.9%, leaving equal black margins at top and bottom. The left panel holds the NVIDIA GTC 2026 logo centred in its upper half above a city illustration; the right panel holds a pyramid of speaker cards in three rows (3 top, 10 middle, 10 bottom) centred over the same city illustration. No title, footer, or slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Left panel: a full-colour 3D rendered night city model (illustration) filling the lower part of the panel, showing glowing white and gold low-rise buildings, dark towers with lit windows, green-lit signage blocks, roads with white lines, and a central green-lit arena, viewed from above at an oblique angle; the NVIDIA GTC logo (green eye emblem #76B900 with white NVIDIA wordmark and white GTC letters, with 2026 in small white type beneath) is placed centrally in the upper half on black; the city fades into black at the top, lit from within. Right panel: the same city illustration fills the background behind the cards; over it, 23 speaker cards arranged in three rows, each a portrait photograph on a dark card with vertical green name text on the left edge and a small green NVIDIA GTC LIVE badge at the bottom; the top three cards are larger (Sarah Guo, Alfred Lin, Gavin Baker) with rank text in green at top right; the other cards are small portraits of individuals in casual or business attire against dark backgrounds. Dominant colours: black #000000, NVIDIA green #76B900, white #FFFFFF, warm gold lights.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 covering the whole slide. Pure black #000000 background with no gradient; panels have thin dark grey #2A2A2A rounded borders with corner radius about 1% of slide width and no shadow. Names and rank labels are in NVIDIA green #76B900 uppercase condensed sans-serif, set vertically; logo wordmark is white bold sans-serif (NVIDIA-style geometric) with GTC in heavy white sans-serif; year 2026 in regular white sans-serif. Body text on cards is very small green and white sans-serif caps. Accent colour is green #76B900.

### Slide 49

**Text**

NVIDIA ♥ DELL Technologies ♥ NTT DATA
“Dell AI Data Platform with NVIDIA helps us build scalable and repeatable data / pipelines that drive automation—processing massive data volumes in / minutes instead of hours and delivering transformative value for our clients.”
— Abhijit Dubey, CEO of NTT DATA
Dell AI Data Platform / Data Orchestration Engine / Data Engines / elastic / Spark / NVIDIA cuVS / NVIDIA cuDF / High Performance Scale-Out Storage / NVIDIA CMX / Data Protection and Cyber Resilience / NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell / Dell AI Factory with NVIDIA
Enterprise Data Processing / From Hours to Minutes / 10TB Dataset
0 20 40 60 80 100 / CPU / GPU / 3X / Faster
NVIDIA ♥ Google Cloud ♥ Snapchat logo
“Our collaboration with NVIDIA and Google Cloud helps us innovate faster for more than a / billion Snapchatters worldwide. By lowering costs and scaling experiments across petabytes / of data, we’re delivering AI-powered experiences more quickly and efficiently.”
— Saral Jain, CIO of Snap
Google Cloud AI Hypercomputer / Flexible Consumption / Open Software / Apache Spark Engine / Apache Spark Jobs / NVIDIA cuDF / Dataproc / Managed Apache Spark and Hadoop / Google Kubernetes Engine / Control plane, APIs, Scheduling and monitoring / Performance-optimized Hardware / Compute / Storage / GCS Rapid / GCS Lustre / Networking / Jupiter Network
A/B Experimentation Platform / 45k CPUs to 1k GPUs
0 10,000 20,000 30,000 40,000 50,000 / CPU / GPU / 76% / Cost Savings

**Layout**

Two identical 16:9 cards sit side by side on a black slide with equal margins of about 4.8% left and right and roughly 18% above and below the cards. Each card is a self-contained panel with its logo row centred at the top, a centred italic quote beneath, then a small diagram on the left and a bar chart on the right. No slide title, footer, or slide number is present.

**Visuals**

Left card: a centred logo row (NVIDIA eye logo and wordmark, red heart, Dell Technologies wordmark with 'Technologies' beneath, red heart, NTT DATA logo). Below the quote is a boxed stack diagram titled 'Dell AI Data Platform' with horizontal bands from top to bottom: white header band, navy band 'Data Orchestration Engine', navy band with 'Data Engines' over elastic and Spark logos and two green boxes 'NVIDIA cuVS' and 'NVIDIA cuDF', navy band 'High Performance Scale-Out Storage' with green box 'NVIDIA CMX', navy band 'Data Protection and Cyber Resilience', green bar 'NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell', and a white footer band 'Dell AI Factory with NVIDIA'. To its right is a vertical bar chart titled 'Enterprise Data Processing / From Hours to Minutes' with subtitle '10TB Dataset'. Y-axis runs 0 to 100 in steps of 20 with no units printed. Two bars: CPU in blue, about 100; GPU in green, about 33 (about, no value printed). A green callout '3X Faster' with a thin downward arrow sits between the bars. Right card: a logo row with NVIDIA eye logo and wordmark, red heart, Google Cloud wordmark, red heart, and the white Snapchat ghost logo. Below the quote is a boxed stack diagram titled 'Google Cloud AI Hypercomputer' with a multicolour Google Cloud icon, white band 'Flexible Consumption', dark panel 'Open Software' containing two grey-outlined boxes 'Apache Spark Engine' and 'Apache Spark Jobs', a green band 'NVIDIA cuDF', two blue boxes 'Dataproc' and 'Google Kubernetes Engine' with small white descriptive text, and a panel 'Performance-optimized Hardware' with three columns: green 'Compute', grey 'Storage' and grey 'Networking'. To its right is a vertical bar chart titled 'A/B Experimentation Platform / 45k CPUs to 1k GPUs'. Y-axis runs 0 to 50,000 in steps of 10,000 with no units. CPU bar in blue about 45,000 (about); GPU bar in green about 1,000 (about), a sliver at the axis. Green callout '76% Cost Savings' with a thin downward arrow pointing at the GPU bar.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 with two dark cards on top. Black #000000 slide. **Layout**

**Visuals**

Two raster screenshots of architecture diagrams, each 1448 by 815 pixels, placed as boxed images. Left panel, titled by NVIDIA logo, heart symbol and CoreWeave logo: a partner row of eight wordmarks (Canva, cohere, CURSOR, mercado libre, Mistral AI, Morgan Stanley, OpenAI, runway) in white and grey; a row of ten gold line icons with labels (W&B Models, W&B Training, W&B Inference, W&B Weave, CoreWeave Inference, CoreWeave Slurm on Kubernetes, CoreWeave Kubernetes Service, CoreWeave Compute, CoreWeave Storage, CoreWeave Mission Control); a box labelled System Builders and OEMs; a platform box with a gold-outlined tab reading NVIDIA AI Platform containing a row of twelve small gold-outlined chips (cuDNN, Magnum IO, cuOpt, cuVS, cuDF, Omniverse, TRT-LLM, Dynamo, NeMo, PhysicsNeMo, Cosmos, Nemotron) above a row of seven hardware pictograms with labels (NVIDIA Hopper, NVIDIA RTX PRO Blackwell Server Edition, NVIDIA Blackwell, Confidential Computing, NVIDIA Rubin, NVIDIA ConnectX and BlueField Networking, NVIDIA Quantum IB, Spectrum-X Networking). **Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 covering the whole slide, with two dark #0A0A0A rounded panels placed on it. Black #000000 slide background; panel cards are near-black #0A0A0A with a thin #2A2A2A border and corner radius about 1 percent of slide width. NVIDIA logo and accent green #76B900; heart symbols red #D7261E; primary label text white #FFFFFF, secondary text light grey #B0B0B0; icon and platform accents gold #F2B705 (left) and blue #2F6FE0 with teal #1FB5A8 and purple #7B5CE6 (right). **Layout**

Two equal 16:9-style cards sit side by side on a black slide, each about 44% of slide width, vertically centred in the middle band (top edge about 18% and bottom edge about 82% of slide height), with roughly 4.8% margins left and right and a gutter of about 2.6% between them. Each card has a centred title block near the upper third and its graphic filling the lower two-thirds. There is no footer, slide number, or logo outside the cards.

**Visuals**

Left card: a horizontal timeline of three circles joined by a thin green hand-drawn line running across the card. Circle 1 (2023) holds the OpenAI blossom/knot logo in teal-green (#10A37F) on a white-outlined circle. Circle 2 (2024) holds the 'o1' logo in cream/gold letters. Circle 3 (2025) holds the 'CLAUDE CODE' wordmark in coral/orange (#D97757) capitals. Each circle is outlined in white with a sketchy hand-drawn stroke, about 7% of slide width in diameter. Labels above circles 2 and 3 read MODELS & CONTEXT 10X / TOKENS 10X and MODELS & CONTEXT 100X / TOKENS 100X in small grey capitals; years sit below each circle. Right card: a bar chart with two green (#76B900) hand-sketched bars on a thin white baseline with tick marks. Bar 1 is short and labelled $0.5T, captioned GTC DC 2025 2025-2026 beneath. A bracket to the right of bar 2 groups ANTHROPIC, METASL, MULTIPLE OSS in green, with a green brace labelled NEW SINCE GTC DC 2025; below it, white labels XAI, GEMINI, OPENAI. A header BLACKWELL + RUBIN sits above the bars. A pie chart to the right is split into a 60% slice on the left and a 40% slice on the right, both filled bright green (#76B900) with dark sketched edges and a white separating line; the 60% and 40% values are printed in dark text on the slices. Leader lines point from CUDA IN CLOUD AI NATIVES (top) to the 60% slice, from NCP SCC SOVEREIGN AI INDUSTRIAL ENTERPRISE (right) to the 40% slice, and from HYPERSCALERS (bottom) to the 60% slice. No axes or numeric scales are printed on the bars.

### Slide 63

**Text**

NVIDIA Dynamo / Input / Prefill / 1st / Token / Remaining / Tokens / KV Cache / Decode / Vera Rubin NVL72 / Groq 3 LPX / KV CACHE / PREFILL / DECODE / ATTN / ACTIVATIONS / DECODE / FFN / TOKENS / Uniting Processors of Extreme FLOPS and Bandwidth / Available 2H26 / NVIDIA Groq 3 LPX / AI Inference Compute / 315 PFLOPS / SRAM Capacity / 128 GB / Memory Bandwidth / 40 PB/s / Scale-Up Density / 256 Chips / Scale-Up Bandwidth / 640 TB/s / LPU C2C Spine Connectors / 8 LPUs / FPGA / Host CPU / BF4 / LPU C2C Links

**Layout**

Two equal dark rounded panels sit side by side, centred on a black slide with a wide black margin above and below. The left panel holds a process diagram with its title NVIDIA Dynamo at the top and a caption centred near the bottom. The right panel has a product photo on the left, a centred title with a five-row spec table on the right, and a tray photo with callout labels below the table. No slide number or footer is visible.

**Visuals**

Left panel: a flow diagram. Input text has an arrow into a Prefill chip (a square dark processor with a grid of small gold-edged cores). From Prefill, one arrow runs right and down to a row of five small squares, the first white and the other four dark grey, labelled 1st Token and Remaining Tokens, with a brace under the four remaining squares. A vertical arrow labelled KV Cache runs down from Prefill to a Decode chip identical in style, and an arrow from Decode runs right to the brace. Next to the diagram are two rack illustrations. Vera Rubin NVL72 is a tall black cabinet with a gold-grey grille, containing a bright green block labelled PREFILL at the top, a gold-grey middle section, and a green block labelled DECODE / ATTN at the bottom. A curved white arrow labelled KV CACHE runs from the rack down and left. Groq 3 LPX is a second tall black cabinet with a bright green column labelled DECODE / FFN. Between the racks, a white arrow labelled ACTIVATIONS points right and a white arrow below it points left. A white arrow labelled TOKENS points right out of the Groq rack. Right panel: a photograph of a tall black server rack with a gold-lit front grille and a partly open side panel, standing on a dark reflective floor, lit from the top, dominant colours black and gold-grey. Below the spec table is a close-up photograph of a gold-grey compute tray, lying flat and angled, with green highlight outlines around the LPU chips at the top, the FPGA and host CPU in the middle, and the C2C link connectors at the bottom left. Thin green leader lines run from each label to its part. The spec table has five rows with labels in the left column and bold gold-white values in the right column: AI Inference Compute 315 PFLOPS; SRAM Capacity 128 GB; Memory Bandwidth 40 PB/s; Scale-Up Density 256 Chips; Scale-Up Bandwidth 640 TB/s.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 across the whole slide. Black background throughout. Title text is light grey-white #E6E6E6 in a clean sans-serif (NVIDIA Sans style, regular weight), about 3.5% of slide height for the right panel title and about 2.6% for the left caption. Body and table labels are light grey #D0D0D0 in regular sans-serif, about 1.6% of slide height. Values are bold gold-tan #C8B88A. Accents are bright green #76B900 for highlight blocks and leader lines. Panels have rounded corners of about 1% of slide width, a thin dark grey #333333 border, and a subtle dark-navy #101A2E glow fading in along the bottom edge. No shadows.

### Slide 64

**Text**

Slide 64 of 76 (left panel): Announcing NVIDIA Vera Rubin NVL72 / Launch Partners / AI Labs / ANTHROPIC / CURSOR / Meta / OpenAI / perplexity / Rack Reset Labs / cohere / Harvey / Mistral AI / OpenEvidence / runway / THINKING MACHINES / Cloud / aws / Google Cloud / Microsoft Azure / ORACLE CLOUD INFRASTRUCTURE / CoreWeave / Crusoe / Lambda / NEBIUS / NSCALE / OEMs and ODMs / DELL Technologies / HPE / Lenovo / Supermicro-style oval logo / AIVRES / ASUS / FOXCONN / GIGABYTE / Inventec / PEGATRON / QCT-style icon / wistron / wiwynn / 10X Perf/W | 3.6 EF NVFP4 | 1.6 PB/s HBM4 | 260 TB/s NVLink6 (right panel): Announcing NVIDIA Vera CPU / Launch Partners / Cloud / Meta / ORACLE CLOUD INFRASTRUCTURE / CoreWeave / Crusoe / Lambda / NEBIUS / NSCALE / logetheroi / OEMs and ODMs / CISCO / DELL Technologies / HPE / Lenovo / oval logo / AIVRES / ASRock Rack / ASUS / COMPAL / FOXCONN / GIGABYTE / hyve / Inventec / MiTAC / MSI / PEGATRON / QCT / wistron / wiwynn / 256 Vera CPUs | 300 TB/s LPDDR5X | ETL Spine | 6.5X Throughput

**Layout**

Two identical 16:9 slide renders sit side by side on a pure black canvas, each inside a thin-bordered rounded rectangle with the slide content filling it. In each slide a rack illustration sits at the left, the title is centred in the middle-right column, and the partner logo groups are stacked in boxed sections to its right; the performance footer line sits bottom-left of each slide, with no slide number visible.

**Visuals**

Left slide: a photograph-style render of a black NVIDIA Vera Rubin NVL72 rack cabinet, open front door showing a glowing gold-tan compute tray stack, plus a single compute tray (blade) lying diagonally at its lower right with gold-edged PCB detail; dark, low-key studio lighting with warm highlights on the tray; the rack is full-height and fully within the frame, not cropped. Right slide: a similar black rack cabinet with open door showing gold-tan stacked trays, shown without the separate blade, same lighting and framing. Both sections contain small logo wordmarks in white or light grey on dark background arranged in rows inside thin grey-outlined boxes with gold-tan section labels; a blue-violet glow along the bottom edge of each slide. The two slide images are boxed (not full-bleed) with rounded corners.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 canvas, with each slide having a near-black background with a dark violet-blue gradient along its bottom edge. Black background (#000000) with a dark navy-to-violet glow along the bottom edge (about #1A1A3A fading to #2B3A8C). Titles in white (#FFFFFF) sans-serif (NVIDIA Sans-like geometric sans, regular weight), title text about 3.0% of slide height (cap height about 2.1%). Section labels AI Labs, Cloud, OEMs and ODMs in muted gold (#C8A96A) small sans, about 1.2% of slide height, centred on the box top border. Logo wordmarks in white (#FFFFFF) and light grey (#D0D0D0), bold. Footer text light grey (#BFBFBF) sans about 1.0% of slide height. Partner boxes have thin grey (#4A4A4A) 1px outlines, square corners; the outer slide frames have thin grey (#333333) borders with about 2% corner radius and no shadow.

**Layout**

Two equal 16:9 slide panels sit side by side on a full black canvas, each with a thin grey rounded border, the left panel spanning about x 4.8 to 48.7 percent and the right about x 51.3 to 95.2 percent, both at y 18.1 to 82 percent. Left panel: rack image on the left third, centred title and two boxed logo grids on the right half, caption along the bottom edge. Right panel: centred title block at the top, a centred spec table, a row of chip icons, a row of tray illustrations and a full-width rack strip along the bottom edge. No slide numbers or footers are visible.

**Visuals**

Left panel: a photograph of a dark server rack with its door open, showing a grid of gold-lit compute trays inside and a second dark rack behind it at right, shot three-quarter view from the front left, lit from within by warm gold light, with a black background and a dark blue glow at the base; a rounded-corner logo grid boxed with thin grey lines containing the Cloud group (two rows of 4 and 4 logos) and the OEMs group (four rows of 4, 4, 4 and 3 logos plus a centred row of 3). Right panel: a centred table with three columns (row label, X86 + Hopper, Vera Rubin) and six rows as listed in text, Vera Rubin column values in gold #D4B26A; a row of seven small chip illustrations (gold and black packages, the Spectrum CPO package is larger with a multicoloured die); a row of six tray illustrations (black chassis with gold edges) with labels beneath; a full-width strip of about 30 dark gold-edged rack units along the bottom edge, cropped at the slide bottom edge.

**Design**

Background black #000000 with a faint dark-blue glow along the bottom of each panel (#0B1A3A). Titles white #FFFFFF in a geometric sans-serif (NVIDIA Sans style), regular weight; body and table text light grey #D0D0D0; accent gold #D4B26A for Vera Rubin column and chip labels; logo group labels Cloud and OEMs in grey #A0A0A0 with thin divider lines #4A4A4A. Left title about 3.2 percent of slide height, body text about 1.2 percent. Right title about 4.2 percent of slide height, subtitle about 2.8 percent. Panels have 0.5 percent corner radius with a 1 px #333333 border; no shadows.

**Layout**

Two identical 16:9 images sit side by side on a black slide, each about 43.9% of slide width and 63.9% of slide height, vertically centred with about 18% margin top and bottom and a gap of about 2.5% between them (left at x 4.8, right at x 51.3). Each image has its title centred near the top (left panel title at about 26% from the top of the image, right panel title centred at about 36% of slide height). No footer or slide number is visible on the slide.

**Visuals**

Left image: a white hand-drawn sketch diagram on a near-black background with a dark navy-to-black gradient at the bottom edge. A central dashed circle labelled NEMOCLAW contains a small circular photo of a smiling man in a dark jacket holding a red-and-orange object. Around the circle, white line arrows (double-headed, curved) connect to: MULTI-MODAL PROMPT (speech-bubble icon at top, arrow pointing down into the circle), FILES STRUCTURED, UNSTRUCTURED (database cylinder icon, arrow to circle), MEMORY (brain icon, curved arrow), COMPUTER USE (monitor-with-chart icon, horizontal double arrow), LLM (arrow to the right panel), TOOLS CLI, MCP (wrench and screwdriver icons, arrow), SUB-AGENTS (cluster of small robot/vehicle icons, arrow), SKILLS (document icon, curved arrow), and OPENSHELL (a wooden tray with colourful objects in a green-outlined box). Left green-outlined rounded box (bright green glow #76B900 outline, about 3 px, black interior) holds cuDF, cuVS (purple 3D cube icon) and vGPU (computer monitor with bar chart icon). Right green-outlined rounded box holds NEMOTRON NEMO DYNAMO NIM (green hexagonal icon), AI-Q (stacked blue chip icon) and cuOPT (wavy brown-and-tan terrain icon). Right image: a dark-grey chart card with a stacked fan of six rounded product cards to its left and right (BioNeMo, Earth-2, Nemotron, Cosmos, GR00T, Alpamayo), each with a small coloured 3D illustration: a green DNA-like helix, a blue-and-green Earth globe, a grey humanoid figure, a spiral galaxy in purple, a red-and-gold robot figure, and a gold-and-blue car. The central chart is a line chart titled NVIDIA is World's Largest Contributor to Open-Source AI, y-axis labelled Repos with ticks 100, 200, 300, 400; x-axis from Jan 2025 to Feb 2026; the NVIDIA line in bright green rises most steeply to about 400 by Feb 2026 with a green logo marker; Alibaba Cloud line in orange-red follows close behind, rising to about 250 (estimate); Hugging Face, Meta, Google and other series in grey, blue, pink and teal stay below about 150 (estimates); legend at upper right lists NVIDIA, Alibaba Cloud, Hugging Face, AI2, Meta, Google, Mistral, Microsoft, Tencent and Zai with coloured dot markers. The chart card has a dark charcoal background (#1E1E1E) with a thin grey border. Below the cards a dark reflective floor fades to black.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 across the entire slide. Black (#000000) slide background with no decorative frame. Panels are not boxed beyond their own internal backgrounds: the left diagram uses a near-black (#0A0A0A) fill with a faint dark navy gradient (#14183A) at the bottom edge. Titles are white (#FFFFFF). Diagram labels are white (#F2F2F2) in uppercase sans-serif. Accent colour is NVIDIA green (#76B900) used for the glowing outlines of the two boxes and for the labels NEMOCLAW, cuDF, cuVS, vGPU, NEMOTRON NEMO DYNAMO NIM, AI-Q, cuOPT and OPENSHELL (bright green #8DDB00). Typeface is a geometric-humanist sans-serif similar to NVIDIA Sans (closest common: Helvetica Neue Medium), regular weight for body labels and medium weight for titles. Left title is about 2.8% of slide height; subtitle about 1.6%; uppercase labels about 1.5%. Lines are white, about 1 px, with rounded arrowheads. Green boxes have rounded corners of about 2% of width and a soft outer green glow.

**Text**

**Layout**

Two dark rounded cards sit side by side on a black slide, each 43.9% of the slide width and vertically centred between about 18% and 82% of the height, with a 2.5% gap between them and equal side margins of about 4.8%. The left card holds a 4-column by 3-row grid of twelve small chart thumbnails, each with a two-line label above it. The right card holds a bar ranking, and its title is centred above the card in the upper part of the slide. There is no footer, slide number or logo.

**Visuals**

Left card, a grid of twelve chart thumbnails in 4 columns and 3 rows, each thumbnail a dark chart screenshot with thin green-yellow axis and text. Row 1: AA Intelligence vs Efficiency (scatter plot, x-axis labelled 'Output Tokens per Second' and y-axis 'Intelligence Index', green-highlighted region and points, no printed values); PinchBench (horizontal bar list of models with green highlighted top bar and grey bars with printed percentages); DeepResearch Bench II (table with rank, model and score rows, top row highlighted green); ViDoRe, MMTEB, BRIGHT (table of model rows with numeric columns, top row highlighted). Row 2: OCRBench v2 (table with header row and numeric columns, highlighted row); AA Conversational Dynamics vs Reasoning (scatter plot with green highlighted region and points, no printed values); Physical AI Bench (table of model rows with numeric cells, first rows highlighted); Physical Reasoning from Video (table with green highlighted first rows). Row 3: LIBERO (table with columns and percentage values, row labels such as Spatial, Object, Goal, Long, Average); MolmoSpaces (list of rows with bar values and logos); RoboArena (table with rank and numeric columns); LingoQA Waymo E2E (ranked table with values starting 71.6 at top). Right card, a titled horizontal bar chart with 13 rows visible, the last row cut off at the bottom edge of the inner panel. Each row has a circular logo at left, then the model name, then a grey rounded bar track with the value printed centred inside: anthropic/claude-sonnet-4.6 86.9% (Anthropic 'A' logo); anthropic/claude-opus-4.6 86.3% (Anthropic logo); openai/gpt-5.4 86.0% (OpenAI logo); nvidia/nemotron-3-super-120b-a12b 85.6% (NVIDIA logo, the only highlighted bar, filled green); anthropic/claude-opus-4.5 85.4% (Anthropic logo); moonshotai/kimi-k2.5 84.8% (Moonshot logo); qwen/qwen3.5-122b-a10b 84.5% (Qwen logo); qwen/qwen3.5-plus-02-15 84.1% (Qwen logo); z-ai/glm-5 84.1% (Z.ai logo); anthropic/claude-sonnet-4.5 83.1% (Anthropic logo); minimax/minimax-m2.1 82.2% (MiniMax logo); deepseek/deepseek-v3.2 81.9% (DeepSeek logo); qwen/qwen3.5-397b-a17b 81.7% (Qwen logo). Bar lengths are proportional, ending near 86.9% for the top row and about 81.7% for the last visible row, with the NVIDIA bar the only green one. No axis lines or gridlines are visible. The card has a dark charcoal inner panel with a thin grey border.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 fill covering the entire slide, with two dark cards placed on top. Black #000000 slide. Left card is a near-black #0D0D0D rounded rectangle with a faint dark blue-purple gradient glow along its bottom edge (about #1B1F3B fading to black) and a thin grey #2A2A2A border. Right card is a very dark #111111 rounded rectangle with a thin #2A2A2A border and a faint blue glow at bottom right; inside it sits a charcoal #1A1A1A panel with a #2E2E2E border and rounded corners. Title text is white #FFFFFF in a clean sans-serif (close to Helvetica or Inter), medium weight, roughly 2.3% of slide height, sized relative to body text of about 1.4% of slide height. Chart labels are light grey #D0D0D0 in a sans-serif. Accent is NVIDIA green #76B900 for the highlighted bar and the top-ranked values. Bar tracks are grey #3A3A3A with rounded ends; value labels are white bold text centred on the bars. Corner radii are about 1% of slide width. No shadows beyond the faint glow.

**Review notes**

**Text**

NVIDIA Nemotron 3 Ultra – Best Open Base Model / 5X Efficiency and Highest Reasoning Accuracy on NVIDIA GB200 NVL72 / GLM / Kimi K2 / Nemotron 3 Ultra / Peak Throughput / Understanding MMLU Pro / Code HumanEval, MBPP / Math GSM8K, Minerva Math / Multilingual Global MMLU, MGSM / The World Building Regional AI With NVIDIA Nemotron / United States / WideLabs / EuroLLM / Linagora / Bielik.ai / SOOFI / Dicta / TII / HUMAIN / BharatGen / Gnani.ai / Sarvam AI / Viettel / NAVER Trillion Labs / Institute of Science Tokyo Stockmark / AI Singapore / Indosat

**Layout**

Two equal dark cards sit side by side on a full black slide, each 43.9% wide and 63.9% tall, with a left margin of 4.8% and a right margin of about 4.8%, vertically centred between 18.1% and 82% of the height. Each card has its title centred at the top with a subtitle beneath and a legend row below that; the left card holds two bar-chart panels side by side, the right card holds a world map with pinned labels. No footer, page number or logo is visible.

**Visuals**

Left card: two grouped bar charts. Chart 1, titled Peak Throughput, has a y-axis labelled X, 1X, 2X, 3X, 4X, 5X (scale 0X to 5X, unit multiples of X) and one category; its bars are GLM about 1X (dark grey), Kimi K2 about 2.5X (silver), Nemotron 3 Ultra about 5X (gold, the highest). Chart 2 has a y-axis from 60% to 90% in 10% steps and four categories: Understanding MMLU Pro (GLM about 65%, Kimi K2 about 69%, Nemotron 3 Ultra about 78%); Code HumanEval, MBPP (GLM about 76%, Kimi K2 about 75%, Nemotron 3 Ultra about 86%); Math GSM8K, Minerva Math (GLM about 72%, Kimi K2 about 80%, Nemotron 3 Ultra about 86%); Multilingual Global MMLU, MGSM (GLM about 83%, Kimi K2 about 84%, Nemotron 3 Ultra about 88%). Bars are vertical with gradient shading, the Nemotron series is brightest gold. Legend at top centre shows three square swatches: dark grey GLM, light grey Kimi K2, gold Nemotron 3 Ultra. Right card: a 3D relief map of the world lit with city lights on black, with a dark purple-to-blue glow at the bottom. Pins with flag icons and dark label boxes mark: United States (flag pin over North America), WideLabs (South America), EuroLLM (Europe), Linagora (France area), Bielik.ai and SOOFI (Poland area, pins with flags), Dicta (Israel area), TII and HUMAIN (Middle East, HUMAIN with green pin), BharatGen and Gnani.ai and Sarvam AI (India), Viettel (Vietnam), NAVER Trillion Labs and Institute of Science Tokyo Stockmark (Japan), AI Singapore and Indosat (Southeast Asia). Small grey coin-stack database icons sit beside several labels.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 covering the whole slide, with the two cards sitting on it. Black background, #000000, with the two cards having near-black #0A0A0A fills, thin grey borders about #2A2A2A and rounded corners of about 2% of card width. Titles are white #FFFFFF in a clean sans-serif (NVIDIA Sans style, regular weight), title height about 3% of slide height, subtitle light grey #C8C8C8 about 1.6% of slide height. Chart axis labels are light grey #A0A0A0 about 1.1% of slide height. Accent colours: gold #B8A572 for Nemotron series, silver #C0C0C0 for Kimi K2, dark grey #3A3A3A for GLM. Map labels use white text on dark rounded label boxes.

**Layout**

Two equal dark panels sit side by side, vertically centred on the black slide, each about 43.9% wide and 63.9% tall, with the left panel at x 4.8% and the right at x 51.3%, leaving a gap of about 2.6% between them. Overall the image is in white line-art on black with no photographs.

### Slide 76

**Text**

NVIDIA / GTC

**Layout**

Full-bleed panoramic photograph fills the entire slide with no margins. The NVIDIA GTC logo sits in the lower-right corner, about 6% from the right edge and 16% from the bottom. No title, footer or slide number.

**Visuals**

Photograph, full-bleed, wide panoramic crop. A night scene in a dense pine forest with tall dark trunks on both sides. At the centre-bottom, a small campfire with orange flames sits on a dirt clearing, with small stylised figures seated on fallen logs around it: a white robot-like figure on the left with a guitar leaning beside it, a small figure with a dog-like companion in the centre, a figure playing an acoustic guitar, and a pale seated humanoid figure on the right. Above the trees, a large spiral galaxy with blue and gold arms fills the upper-centre sky against a purple starfield. Lower foreground is almost black with scattered small glowing specks, possibly reflections or fireflies. Lighting is firelight from below and the galaxy glow from above. Dominant colours are near-black, deep violet, blue and orange. Not boxed; the image runs edge to edge, with a dark vignette toward the corners. The NVIDIA GTC logo overlays the lower right of the photo.

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed dark forest night photograph, dominated by near-black #050508 with violet sky #3B2A5C at top. Dark, cinematic night-scene treatment with no text colour scheme. The logo uses a bright green mark (#76B900) with white wordmark letters (#FFFFFF) in a bold geometric sans-serif, with 'NVIDIA' set above 'GTC' in larger, heavier letters. No title or body typography. Photograph has no border, radius or shadow.
